"""Tests for ComorbidityCategory."""

from __future__ import annotations

import pytest

from cranioz.domain.patient.enums.comorbidity_category import (
    ComorbidityCategory,
)


def test_members() -> None:
    expected = {
        "CARDIOVASCULAR",
        "RESPIRATORY",
        "GASTROINTESTINAL",
        "HEMATOLOGICAL",
        "ENDOCRINE",
        "RENAL",
        "HEPATIC",
        "NEUROLOGICAL",
        "MUSCULOSKELETAL",
        "INFECTIOUS_DISEASES",
        "OTHERS",
    }
    assert {m.name for m in ComorbidityCategory} == expected


def test_values() -> None:
    assert ComorbidityCategory.CARDIOVASCULAR.value == "cardiovascular"
    assert ComorbidityCategory.INFECTIOUS_DISEASES.value == "infectious_diseases"
    assert ComorbidityCategory.OTHERS.value == "others"


def test_lookup_by_value() -> None:
    assert ComorbidityCategory("cardiovascular") is ComorbidityCategory.CARDIOVASCULAR
    assert ComorbidityCategory("others") is ComorbidityCategory.OTHERS


def test_unknown_value_raises() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        ComorbidityCategory("oncology")


def test_others_is_last_member() -> None:
    """OTHERS is the escape hatch and is listed last.

    The UI iterates over the categories in declaration order and
    presents OTHERS as the final option. This test documents and
    guards that ordering.
    """
    members = list(ComorbidityCategory)
    assert members[-1] is ComorbidityCategory.OTHERS

"""Tests for DocumentType."""

from __future__ import annotations

import pytest

from cranioz.domain.patient.enums.document_type import DocumentType


def test_members() -> None:
    expected = {
        "NATIONAL_ID",
        "TAX_ID",
        "DRIVERS_LICENSE",
        "PASSPORT",
        "BIRTH_CERTIFICATE",
        "OTHER",
    }
    assert {m.name for m in DocumentType} == expected


def test_values() -> None:
    assert DocumentType.NATIONAL_ID.value == "national_id"
    assert DocumentType.TAX_ID.value == "tax_id"
    assert DocumentType.DRIVERS_LICENSE.value == "drivers_license"
    assert DocumentType.PASSPORT.value == "passport"
    assert DocumentType.BIRTH_CERTIFICATE.value == "birth_certificate"
    assert DocumentType.OTHER.value == "other"


def test_lookup_by_value() -> None:
    assert DocumentType("national_id") is DocumentType.NATIONAL_ID
    assert DocumentType("passport") is DocumentType.PASSPORT


def test_unknown_value_raises() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        DocumentType("unknown")


def test_no_country_specific_values() -> None:
    """Document types are country-agnostic.

    Country-specific names (RG, CPF, SSN, NIF, ...) are resolved by
    the i18n layer from the ``(type, country)`` pair. They must not
    appear as enum values in the domain, to keep the persisted
    representation stable across countries.
    """
    forbidden = {"rg", "cpf", "cnh", "ssn", "nif", "dni"}
    actual = {m.value for m in DocumentType}
    assert forbidden.isdisjoint(actual)



======================================================================
FILE: tests/unit/domain/patient/enums/test_patient_race.py
======================================================================

"""Tests for PatientRace."""

from __future__ import annotations

import pytest

from cranioz.domain.patient.enums.patient_race import PatientRace


def test_members() -> None:
    expected = {
        "WHITE",
        "BROWN",
        "BLACK",
        "YELLOW",
        "INDIGENOUS",
        "NOT_DECLARED",
    }
    assert {m.name for m in PatientRace} == expected


def test_values() -> None:
    assert PatientRace.WHITE.value == "white"
    assert PatientRace.BROWN.value == "brown"
    assert PatientRace.BLACK.value == "black"
    assert PatientRace.YELLOW.value == "yellow"
    assert PatientRace.INDIGENOUS.value == "indigenous"
    assert PatientRace.NOT_DECLARED.value == "not_declared"


def test_lookup_by_value() -> None:
    assert PatientRace("white") is PatientRace.WHITE
    assert PatientRace("brown") is PatientRace.BROWN
    assert PatientRace("not_declared") is PatientRace.NOT_DECLARED


def test_unknown_value_raises() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        PatientRace("unknown")


def test_values_are_stable_identifiers() -> None:
    """Values are stable identifiers for persistence, not labels.

    The user-facing labels ("Branca", "Parda", ...) are resolved by
    the i18n layer, keyed by these values. If a value ever changes,
    persisted records break. This test guards against accidental
    renames.
    """
    expected = {
        PatientRace.WHITE: "white",
        PatientRace.BROWN: "brown",
        PatientRace.BLACK: "black",
        PatientRace.YELLOW: "yellow",
        PatientRace.INDIGENOUS: "indigenous",
        PatientRace.NOT_DECLARED: "not_declared",
    }
    for member, value in expected.items():
        assert member.value == value



======================================================================
FILE: tests/unit/domain/patient/enums/test_patient_sex.py
======================================================================

"""Tests for PatientSex."""

from __future__ import annotations

import pytest

from cranioz.domain.patient.enums.patient_sex import PatientSex


def test_members() -> None:
    expected = {"MALE", "FEMALE", "OTHER"}
    assert {m.name for m in PatientSex} == expected


def test_values() -> None:
    assert PatientSex.MALE.value == "male"
    assert PatientSex.FEMALE.value == "female"
    assert PatientSex.OTHER.value == "other"


def test_lookup_by_value() -> None:
    assert PatientSex("male") is PatientSex.MALE
    assert PatientSex("female") is PatientSex.FEMALE
    assert PatientSex("other") is PatientSex.OTHER


def test_unknown_value_raises() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        PatientSex("nonbinary")


def test_no_unknown_member() -> None:
    """Sex has no UNKNOWN member.

    When the sex is not recorded, the field is ``None`` at the
    domain level, not a sentinel enum value. This keeps "not
    recorded" distinct from "recorded as OTHER".
    """
    assert not hasattr(PatientSex, "UNKNOWN")

    assert PhoneKind.WORK.value == "work"
    assert PhoneKind.OTHER.value == "other"

    assert PhoneKind("other") is PhoneKind.OTHER

FILE: tests/unit/domain/patient/sections/test_address.py

"""Tests for PatientAddress (§4 Endereço).

PatientAddress carries the patient's address. Every field is
optional and free-form; the domain does not validate any format.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import dataclasses

import pytest

from cranioz.domain.patient.sections.address import PatientAddress


def test_address_defaults() -> None:
    a = PatientAddress()
    assert a.postal_code is None
    assert a.street is None
    assert a.number is None
    assert a.complement is None
    assert a.district is None
    assert a.city is None
    assert a.state is None
    assert a.country is None


def test_address_holds_all_fields() -> None:
    a = PatientAddress(
        postal_code="66000-000",
        street="Rua das Flores",
        number="123",
        complement="Apto 45",
        district="Centro",
        city="Belém",
        state="PA",
        country="Brazil",
    )
    assert a.postal_code == "66000-000"
    assert a.city == "Belém"


def test_address_is_frozen() -> None:
    a = PatientAddress()
    attr = "city"
    with pytest.raises(dataclasses.FrozenInstanceError):
        setattr(a, attr, "X")


def test_address_accepts_arbitrary_format() -> None:
    # The domain does not validate postal codes or any other format.
    a = PatientAddress(postal_code="1234", country="XX")
    assert a.postal_code == "1234"


def test_address_equality() -> None:
    a = PatientAddress(city="Belém")
    b = PatientAddress(city="Belém")
    assert a == b


def test_address_with_different_cities_not_equal() -> None:
    a = PatientAddress(city="Belém")
    b = PatientAddress(city="São Paulo")
    assert a != b



======================================================================
FILE: tests/unit/domain/patient/sections/test_antecedents.py
======================================================================

"""Tests for PatientAntecedents (§8 Antecedentes).

PatientAntecedents carries the patient's history beyond the current
complaint, organized in four subsections: surgical history,
devices/implants, family history, and women's health (conditional).

All fields are free-form text. The women's health fields are
meaningful only when the patient's sex is FEMALE; that rule is
enforced by the Patient aggregate, not by this section. This
section only validates that each field is a string.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import dataclasses

import pytest

from cranioz.domain.patient.sections.antecedents import PatientAntecedents


def test_antecedents_defaults_are_empty_strings() -> None:
    a = PatientAntecedents()
    assert a.previous_surgeries == ""
    assert a.anesthesia_history == ""
    assert a.prostheses_implants == ""
    assert a.family_history == ""
    assert a.pregnancy_contraception == ""
    assert a.breastfeeding == ""


def test_antecedents_holds_all_fields() -> None:
    a = PatientAntecedents(
        previous_surgeries="Appendectomy 2010",
        anesthesia_history="No adverse events",
        prostheses_implants="Titanium plate on left femur",
        family_history="Father with hypertension",
        pregnancy_contraception="",
        breastfeeding="",
    )
    assert a.previous_surgeries == "Appendectomy 2010"
    assert a.prostheses_implants == "Titanium plate on left femur"
    assert a.family_history == "Father with hypertension"


def test_antecedents_holds_womens_health_fields() -> None:
    a = PatientAntecedents(
        pregnancy_contraception="Not currently pregnant",
        breastfeeding="No",
    )
    assert a.pregnancy_contraception == "Not currently pregnant"
    assert a.breastfeeding == "No"


def test_antecedents_does_not_enforce_womens_health_rule() -> None:
    """The section does not know the patient's sex.

    The rule "women's health fields must be empty when the patient
    is not female" is enforced by the Patient aggregate. This
    section only validates individual fields. This test documents
    that the section accepts any combination of the fields.
    """
    a = PatientAntecedents(pregnancy_contraception="X")
    assert a.pregnancy_contraception == "X"


def test_antecedents_is_frozen() -> None:
    a = PatientAntecedents()
    attr = "previous_surgeries"
    with pytest.raises(dataclasses.FrozenInstanceError):
        setattr(a, attr, "X")


def test_antecedents_equality() -> None:
    a = PatientAntecedents(previous_surgeries="X")
    b = PatientAntecedents(previous_surgeries="X")
    assert a == b


def test_antecedents_with_different_values_not_equal() -> None:
    a = PatientAntecedents(previous_surgeries="X")
    b = PatientAntecedents(previous_surgeries="Y")
    assert a != b



======================================================================
FILE: tests/unit/domain/patient/sections/test_comorbidities.py
======================================================================

"""Tests for PatientComorbidities (§6 Comorbidades).

PatientComorbidities carries eleven free-form text fields, one per
comorbidity category.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import dataclasses

import pytest

from cranioz.domain.patient.enums.comorbidity_category import (
    ComorbidityCategory,
)
from cranioz.domain.patient.sections.comorbidities import (
    PatientComorbidities,
)

# All eleven field names, in the same set as ComorbidityCategory.
_ALL_FIELDS = {
    "cardiovascular",
    "respiratory",
    "gastrointestinal",
    "hematological",
    "endocrine",
    "renal",
    "hepatic",
    "neurological",
    "musculoskeletal",
    "infectious_diseases",
    "others",
}


def test_comorbidities_defaults_are_empty_strings() -> None:
    c = PatientComorbidities()
    for field_name in _ALL_FIELDS:
        assert getattr(c, field_name) == ""


def test_comorbidities_holds_all_fields() -> None:
    c = PatientComorbidities(
        cardiovascular="Hypertension",
        respiratory="Asthma",
        gastrointestinal="",
        hematological="",
        endocrine="Diabetes type 2",
        renal="",
        hepatic="",
        neurological="",
        musculoskeletal="",
        infectious_diseases="",
        others="",
    )
    assert c.cardiovascular == "Hypertension"
    assert c.respiratory == "Asthma"
    assert c.endocrine == "Diabetes type 2"


def test_comorbidities_is_frozen() -> None:
    c = PatientComorbidities()
    attr = "cardiovascular"
    with pytest.raises(dataclasses.FrozenInstanceError):
        setattr(c, attr, "X")


def test_comorbidities_equality() -> None:
    a = PatientComorbidities(cardiovascular="X")
    b = PatientComorbidities(cardiovascular="X")
    assert a == b


def test_comorbidities_with_different_values_not_equal() -> None:
    a = PatientComorbidities(cardiovascular="X")
    b = PatientComorbidities(cardiovascular="Y")
    assert a != b


def test_fields_match_comorbidity_categories() -> None:
    """Every category has a corresponding field, and vice versa.

    The enum and the section are kept in sync: adding a category
    requires adding a field. This test guards against drift.
    """
    category_values = {c.value for c in ComorbidityCategory}
    assert category_values == _ALL_FIELDS

======================================================================
FILE: tests/unit/domain/patient/sections/test_documents.py
======================================================================

"""Tests for PatientDocuments (§2 Documentos e Contato).

PatientDocuments carries the patient's identity document, phone
numbers, email, and health insurance data. It enforces two groups
of conditional invariants:

- Document coherence: type, country, and number must be set
  together, or none of them set.
- Insurance coherence: name and card number must be set together,
  or none of them set.

The tests do not depend on Qt, filesystem, or infrastructure.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import dataclasses

import pytest

from cranioz.domain.patient.enums.document_type import DocumentType
from cranioz.domain.patient.enums.phone_kind import PhoneKind
from cranioz.domain.patient.sections.documents import PatientDocuments
from cranioz.domain.patient.value_objects.phone_number import PhoneNumber

# ----- Construction -------------------------------------------------


def test_documents_defaults() -> None:
    docs = PatientDocuments()
    assert docs.document_type is None
    assert docs.document_country is None
    assert docs.document_number is None
    assert docs.phone_primary is None
    assert docs.phone_secondary is None
    assert docs.email is None
    assert docs.health_insurance is None
    assert docs.insurance_card_number is None
    assert docs.notes == ""


def test_documents_with_all_fields() -> None:
    docs = PatientDocuments(
        document_type=DocumentType.TAX_ID,
        document_country="BR",
        document_number="123.456.789-00",
        phone_primary=PhoneNumber("+55 91 99582-8523"),
        phone_secondary=PhoneNumber("(91) 3242-1234", PhoneKind.HOME),
        email="maria@example.com",
        health_insurance="Unimed",
        insurance_card_number="1234567890",
        notes="Additional notes.",
    )
    assert docs.document_type is DocumentType.TAX_ID
    assert docs.document_country == "BR"
    assert docs.document_number == "123.456.789-00"
    assert docs.email == "maria@example.com"
    assert docs.health_insurance == "Unimed"


def test_documents_is_frozen() -> None:
    docs = PatientDocuments()
    attr = "notes"
    with pytest.raises(dataclasses.FrozenInstanceError):
        setattr(docs, attr, "changed")


# ----- Validation: document coherence ------------------------------


def test_document_type_without_country_raises() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="document_country"):
        PatientDocuments(
            document_type=DocumentType.NATIONAL_ID,
            document_number="12345",
        )


def test_document_type_without_number_raises() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="document_number"):
        PatientDocuments(
            document_type=DocumentType.NATIONAL_ID,
            document_country="BR",
        )


def test_document_country_without_type_raises() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="document_country"):
        PatientDocuments(document_country="BR")


def test_document_number_without_type_raises() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="document_number"):
        PatientDocuments(document_number="12345")


def test_document_country_must_be_two_letters() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="ISO 3166-1"):
        PatientDocuments(
            document_type=DocumentType.NATIONAL_ID,
            document_country="BRA",
            document_number="12345",
        )


def test_document_country_must_be_uppercase() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="ISO 3166-1"):
        PatientDocuments(
            document_type=DocumentType.NATIONAL_ID,
            document_country="br",
            document_number="12345",
        )


def test_document_country_must_be_ascii() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="ISO 3166-1"):
        PatientDocuments(
            document_type=DocumentType.NATIONAL_ID,
            document_country="B\u00c7",  # "BÇ"
            document_number="12345",
        )


def test_document_number_whitespace_only_raises() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="whitespace"):
        PatientDocuments(
            document_type=DocumentType.NATIONAL_ID,
            document_country="BR",
            document_number="   ",
        )


# ----- Validation: insurance coherence -----------------------------


def test_insurance_without_card_number_raises() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="insurance_card_number"):
        PatientDocuments(health_insurance="Unimed")


def test_card_number_without_insurance_raises() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="insurance_card_number"):
        PatientDocuments(insurance_card_number="12345")


def test_insurance_whitespace_only_raises() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="whitespace"):
        PatientDocuments(
            health_insurance="   ",
            insurance_card_number="12345",
        )


def test_card_number_whitespace_only_raises() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="whitespace"):
        PatientDocuments(
            health_insurance="Unimed",
            insurance_card_number="   ",
        )


# ----- Equality -----------------------------------------------------


def test_documents_equality_by_value() -> None:
    a = PatientDocuments(notes="x")
    b = PatientDocuments(notes="x")
    assert a == b


def test_documents_with_different_notes_not_equal() -> None:
    a = PatientDocuments(notes="x")
    b = PatientDocuments(notes="y")
    assert a != b

    assert f.mother_name is None

    assert f.mother_name == "Maria Silva"

    b = PatientFiliation(father_name="Y")
    assert a != b

======================================================================
FILE: tests/unit/domain/patient/sections/test_identity.py
======================================================================

"""Tests for PatientIdentity (§1 Identificação).

PatientIdentity is a frozen value object carrying the patient's
name, birth date, sex, race, nationality, blood type, and optional
photo reference. These tests exercise construction, defaults,
validation, immutability, and equality.

The tests do not depend on Qt, filesystem, or infrastructure.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import dataclasses
from datetime import date

import pytest

from cranioz.domain.patient.enums.blood_type import BloodType
from cranioz.domain.patient.enums.patient_race import PatientRace
from cranioz.domain.patient.enums.patient_sex import PatientSex
from cranioz.domain.patient.sections.identity import PatientIdentity

# ----- Helpers ------------------------------------------------------


def _identity(**overrides) -> PatientIdentity:
    base: dict = {"full_name": "Gabriel Silva"}
    base.update(overrides)
    return PatientIdentity(**base)


# ----- Construction -------------------------------------------------


def test_identity_holds_full_name() -> None:
    identity = _identity()
    assert identity.full_name == "Gabriel Silva"


def test_identity_defaults() -> None:
    identity = _identity()
    assert identity.social_name is None
    assert identity.birth_date is None
    assert identity.sex is None
    assert identity.race is PatientRace.NOT_DECLARED
    assert identity.nationality is None
    assert identity.blood_type is BloodType.UNKNOWN
    assert identity.photo_reference is None


def test_identity_holds_all_fields() -> None:
    identity = PatientIdentity(
        full_name="Gabriel Silva",
        social_name="Gabi",
        birth_date=date(1990, 5, 12),
        sex=PatientSex.MALE,
        race=PatientRace.BROWN,
        nationality="Brazil",
        blood_type=BloodType.O_POSITIVE,
        photo_reference="patient/photo/portrait",
    )
    assert identity.social_name == "Gabi"
    assert identity.birth_date == date(1990, 5, 12)
    assert identity.sex is PatientSex.MALE
    assert identity.race is PatientRace.BROWN
    assert identity.nationality == "Brazil"
    assert identity.blood_type is BloodType.O_POSITIVE
    assert identity.photo_reference == "patient/photo/portrait"


def test_identity_is_frozen() -> None:
    identity = _identity()
    attr = "full_name"
    with pytest.raises(dataclasses.FrozenInstanceError):
        setattr(identity, attr, "Other")


# ----- Validation: full_name ---------------------------------------


def test_empty_full_name_raises() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="full_name"):
        _identity(full_name="")


def test_whitespace_full_name_raises() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="whitespace"):
        _identity(full_name="   ")


# ----- Validation: social_name -------------------------------------


def test_social_name_can_be_none() -> None:
    identity = _identity(social_name=None)
    assert identity.social_name is None


def test_empty_social_name_raises() -> None:
    """An empty string is not a valid social name.

    ``social_name`` is optional. Absence is expressed as ``None``,
    not as an empty string. If a value is present, it must be a
    non-empty, non-whitespace name.
    """
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="social_name"):
        _identity(social_name="")


def test_whitespace_social_name_raises() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="social_name"):
        _identity(social_name="   ")


# ----- Validation: photo_reference ----------------------------------


def test_photo_reference_can_be_none() -> None:
    identity = _identity(photo_reference=None)
    assert identity.photo_reference is None


def test_empty_photo_reference_raises() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="photo_reference"):
        _identity(photo_reference="")


def test_whitespace_photo_reference_raises() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="photo_reference"):
        _identity(photo_reference="   ")


# ----- Equality -----------------------------------------------------


def test_identity_equality_by_value() -> None:
    a = _identity()
    b = _identity()
    assert a == b


def test_identity_with_different_names_not_equal() -> None:
    a = _identity(full_name="A")
    b = _identity(full_name="B")
    assert a != b

FILE: tests/unit/domain/patient/value_objects/test_patient_id.py

"""Tests for PatientId.

PatientId is a frozen value object that identifies a Patient
within the CranioZ application. These tests exercise its
validation, equality, hashing, and representation.

The tests do not depend on Qt, filesystem, or infrastructure.
They run in pure Python.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import dataclasses

import pytest

from cranioz.domain.patient.value_objects.patient_id import PatientId

# ----- Construction -------------------------------------------------


def test_patient_id_holds_value() -> None:
    pid = PatientId("abc-123")
    assert pid.value == "abc-123"


def test_patient_id_is_frozen() -> None:
    pid = PatientId("abc")
    attr = "value"
    with pytest.raises(dataclasses.FrozenInstanceError):
        setattr(pid, attr, "other")


# ----- Validation ---------------------------------------------------


def test_empty_value_raises() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="must not be empty"):
        PatientId("")


def test_whitespace_only_value_raises() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="whitespace"):
        PatientId("   ")


def test_tab_and_newline_value_raises() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="whitespace"):
        PatientId("\t\n")


def test_value_with_surrounding_whitespace_is_accepted() -> None:
    # The domain does not trim; it only rejects values that are
    # entirely whitespace. A value with meaningful content and
    # surrounding spaces is preserved as-is.
    pid = PatientId("  abc  ")
    assert pid.value == "  abc  "


# ----- Equality -----------------------------------------------------


def test_equality_by_value() -> None:
    a = PatientId("abc")
    b = PatientId("abc")
    assert a == b


def test_different_ids_are_not_equal() -> None:
    assert PatientId("abc") != PatientId("xyz")


def test_equality_with_other_type_returns_false() -> None:
    assert PatientId("abc") != "abc"
    assert PatientId("abc") != 123


# ----- Hashing ------------------------------------------------------


def test_patient_id_is_hashable() -> None:
    # Frozen dataclasses are hashable, which makes PatientId usable
    # as a dict key. This matters for the Project domain, which
    # references patients by id.
    d = {PatientId("a"): 1, PatientId("b"): 2}
    assert d[PatientId("a")] == 1


def test_equal_ids_have_equal_hashes() -> None:
    a = PatientId("abc")
    b = PatientId("abc")
    assert hash(a) == hash(b)


# ----- Representation -----------------------------------------------


def test_str_returns_value() -> None:
    assert str(PatientId("abc-123")) == "abc-123"


def test_repr_mentions_value() -> None:
    text = repr(PatientId("abc-123"))
    assert "abc-123" in text
    assert "PatientId" in text



======================================================================
FILE: tests/unit/domain/patient/value_objects/test_phone_number.py
======================================================================

"""Tests for PhoneNumber.

PhoneNumber is a frozen value object combining a phone number with
its kind. These tests exercise construction, validation, equality,
hashing, defaults, and representation.

The tests do not depend on Qt, filesystem, or infrastructure.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import dataclasses

import pytest

from cranioz.domain.patient.enums.phone_kind import PhoneKind
from cranioz.domain.patient.value_objects.phone_number import PhoneNumber

# ----- Construction -------------------------------------------------


def test_phone_number_holds_fields() -> None:
    phone = PhoneNumber(number="+55 91 99582-8523", kind=PhoneKind.MOBILE)
    assert phone.number == "+55 91 99582-8523"
    assert phone.kind is PhoneKind.MOBILE


def test_default_kind_is_mobile() -> None:
    phone = PhoneNumber(number="+55 91 99582-8523")
    assert phone.kind is PhoneKind.MOBILE


def test_number_is_free_form_string() -> None:
    # The domain does not enforce a format.
    variants = [
        "1234",
        "(91) 3242-1234",
        "+55 91 99582-8523",
        "91 9 9582 8523",
    ]
    for value in variants:
        phone = PhoneNumber(number=value)
        assert phone.number == value


# ----- Validation ---------------------------------------------------


def test_empty_number_raises() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="must not be empty"):
        PhoneNumber(number="")


def test_whitespace_only_number_raises() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="whitespace"):
        PhoneNumber(number="   ")


def test_tab_and_newline_number_raises() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="whitespace"):
        PhoneNumber(number="\t\n")


# ----- Immutability -------------------------------------------------


def test_phone_number_is_frozen() -> None:
    phone = PhoneNumber(number="1234")
    attr = "number"
    with pytest.raises(dataclasses.FrozenInstanceError):
        setattr(phone, attr, "5678")


# ----- Equality and hashing ----------------------------------------


def test_equality_by_value() -> None:
    a = PhoneNumber(number="1234", kind=PhoneKind.HOME)
    b = PhoneNumber(number="1234", kind=PhoneKind.HOME)
    assert a == b


def test_equality_considers_kind() -> None:
    a = PhoneNumber(number="1234", kind=PhoneKind.MOBILE)
    b = PhoneNumber(number="1234", kind=PhoneKind.HOME)
    assert a != b


def test_different_number_is_not_equal() -> None:
    a = PhoneNumber(number="1234")
    b = PhoneNumber(number="5678")
    assert a != b


def test_phone_number_is_hashable() -> None:
    a = PhoneNumber(number="1234", kind=PhoneKind.WORK)
    b = PhoneNumber(number="1234", kind=PhoneKind.WORK)
    assert hash(a) == hash(b)


# ----- Kinds --------------------------------------------------------


def test_all_phone_kinds_are_accepted() -> None:
    for kind in PhoneKind:
        phone = PhoneNumber(number="1234", kind=kind)
        assert phone.kind is kind

FILE: tests/unit/domain/project/test_project.py

"""Tests for Project."""

from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import UTC, datetime

import pytest

from cranioz.domain.project.project import Project
from cranioz.domain.project.project_id import ProjectId
from cranioz.domain.project.project_metadata import ProjectMetadata
from cranioz.domain.project.project_status import ProjectStatus

# ----- Helpers ------------------------------------------------------


def _now() -> datetime:
    return datetime(2026, 1, 1, 12, 0, tzinfo=UTC)


def _metadata(project_id: str = "p-001") -> ProjectMetadata:
    return ProjectMetadata(
        project_id=ProjectId(project_id),
        project_name="Case 001",
        patient_id="pat-001",
        patient_display_name="Gabriel Silva",
        clinical_procedure="orthognathic",
        status=ProjectStatus.ACTIVE,
        created_at=_now(),
        updated_at=_now(),
        last_opened_at=None,
        schema_version=1,
    )


def _project(project_id: str = "p-001") -> Project:
    pid = ProjectId(project_id)
    return Project(project_id=pid, metadata=_metadata(project_id))


# ----- Construction -------------------------------------------------


def test_project_holds_id_and_metadata() -> None:
    p = _project()
    assert p.project_id == ProjectId("p-001")
    assert p.metadata.project_id == ProjectId("p-001")
    assert p.metadata.project_name == "Case 001"


def test_project_extras_default_to_empty_dict() -> None:
    p = _project()
    assert p.extras == {}


def test_metadata_project_id_mismatch_raises() -> None:
    pid = ProjectId("p-001")
    wrong_md = _metadata("p-999")
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="must match"):
        Project(project_id=pid, metadata=wrong_md)


# ----- update_metadata ----------------------------------------------


def test_update_metadata_replaces() -> None:
    p = _project()
    later = datetime(2026, 2, 1, tzinfo=UTC)
    new_md = p.metadata.with_name("Renamed", now=later)

    p.update_metadata(new_md)

    assert p.metadata.project_name == "Renamed"
    assert p.metadata.updated_at == later


def test_update_metadata_wrong_project_raises() -> None:
    p = _project()
    other_md = _metadata("p-999")
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="different project"):
        p.update_metadata(other_md)


# ----- Representation -----------------------------------------------


def test_repr_mentions_key_fields() -> None:
    p = _project()
    text = repr(p)
    assert "p-001" in text
    assert "Case 001" in text
    assert "active" in text

======================================================================
FILE: tests/unit/domain/project/test_project_metadata.py
======================================================================

"""Tests for ProjectMetadata."""

from __future__ import annotations

import dataclasses
from datetime import UTC, datetime

import pytest

from cranioz.domain.project.project_id import ProjectId
from cranioz.domain.project.project_metadata import ProjectMetadata
from cranioz.domain.project.project_status import ProjectStatus

# ----- Helpers ------------------------------------------------------


def _now() -> datetime:
    return datetime(2026, 1, 1, 12, 0, tzinfo=UTC)


def _metadata(**overrides) -> ProjectMetadata:
    base: dict = {
        "project_id": ProjectId("p-001"),
        "project_name": "Case 001",
        "patient_id": "pat-001",
        "patient_display_name": "Gabriel Silva",
        "clinical_procedure": "orthognathic",
        "status": ProjectStatus.ACTIVE,
        "created_at": _now(),
        "updated_at": _now(),
        "last_opened_at": None,
        "schema_version": 1,
    }
    base.update(overrides)
    return ProjectMetadata(**base)


# ----- Construction -------------------------------------------------


def test_metadata_holds_all_fields() -> None:
    md = _metadata()
    assert md.project_id == ProjectId("p-001")
    assert md.project_name == "Case 001"
    assert md.patient_id == "pat-001"
    assert md.patient_display_name == "Gabriel Silva"
    assert md.clinical_procedure == "orthognathic"
    assert md.status is ProjectStatus.ACTIVE
    assert md.schema_version == 1


def test_metadata_is_frozen() -> None:
    md = _metadata()
    attr = "project_name"
    with pytest.raises(dataclasses.FrozenInstanceError):
        setattr(md, attr, "Other")


def test_empty_project_name_raises() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="project_name"):
        _metadata(project_name="")


def test_empty_patient_id_raises() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="patient_id"):
        _metadata(patient_id="")


def test_empty_patient_display_name_raises() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="patient_display_name"):
        _metadata(patient_display_name="")


def test_schema_version_zero_raises() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="schema_version"):
        _metadata(schema_version=0)


def test_schema_version_negative_raises() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="schema_version"):
        _metadata(schema_version=-1)


def test_updated_before_created_raises() -> None:
    earlier = datetime(2025, 1, 1, tzinfo=UTC)
    later = datetime(2026, 1, 1, tzinfo=UTC)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="updated_at"):
        _metadata(created_at=later, updated_at=earlier)


def test_last_opened_before_created_raises() -> None:
    earlier = datetime(2025, 1, 1, tzinfo=UTC)
    later = datetime(2026, 1, 1, tzinfo=UTC)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="last_opened_at"):
        _metadata(
            created_at=later,
            updated_at=later,
            last_opened_at=earlier,
        )


def test_naive_created_at_raises() -> None:
    naive = datetime(2026, 1, 1)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="created_at"):
        _metadata(created_at=naive, updated_at=naive)


def test_naive_updated_at_raises() -> None:
    naive = datetime(2026, 1, 1)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="updated_at"):
        _metadata(created_at=_now(), updated_at=naive)


def test_naive_last_opened_at_raises() -> None:
    naive = datetime(2026, 1, 1)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="last_opened_at"):
        _metadata(last_opened_at=naive)


# ----- Mutators -----------------------------------------------------


def test_with_status_returns_new_instance() -> None:
    md = _metadata()
    later = datetime(2026, 2, 1, tzinfo=UTC)
    new_md = md.with_status(ProjectStatus.ARCHIVED, now=later)

    assert new_md is not md
    assert new_md.status is ProjectStatus.ARCHIVED
    assert new_md.updated_at == later
    # Original unchanged.
    assert md.status is ProjectStatus.ACTIVE


def test_with_name_returns_new_instance() -> None:
    md = _metadata()
    later = datetime(2026, 2, 1, tzinfo=UTC)
    new_md = md.with_name("Renamed", now=later)

    assert new_md.project_name == "Renamed"
    assert new_md.updated_at == later
    assert md.project_name == "Case 001"


def test_with_name_empty_raises() -> None:
    md = _metadata()
    later = datetime(2026, 2, 1, tzinfo=UTC)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="project_name"):
        md.with_name("", now=later)


def test_with_last_opened_sets_timestamp() -> None:
    md = _metadata()
    later = datetime(2026, 3, 1, tzinfo=UTC)
    new_md = md.with_last_opened(now=later)

    assert new_md.last_opened_at == later
    assert md.last_opened_at is None

"""Tests for ProjectStatus."""

from __future__ import annotations

from cranioz.domain.project.project_status import ProjectStatus


def test_members() -> None:
    expected = {"DRAFT", "ACTIVE", "ARCHIVED"}
    assert {m.name for m in ProjectStatus} == expected


def test_values() -> None:
    assert ProjectStatus.DRAFT.value == "draft"
    assert ProjectStatus.ACTIVE.value == "active"
    assert ProjectStatus.ARCHIVED.value == "archived"


def test_lookup_by_value() -> None:
    assert ProjectStatus("draft") is ProjectStatus.DRAFT
    assert ProjectStatus("active") is ProjectStatus.ACTIVE
    assert ProjectStatus("archived") is ProjectStatus.ARCHIVED

FILE: tests/unit/domain/project/test_project_summary.py

"""Tests for ProjectSummary."""

from __future__ import annotations

import dataclasses
from datetime import UTC, datetime

import pytest

from cranioz.domain.project.project_id import ProjectId
from cranioz.domain.project.project_metadata import ProjectMetadata
from cranioz.domain.project.project_status import ProjectStatus
from cranioz.domain.project.project_summary import ProjectSummary

# ----- Helpers ------------------------------------------------------


def _now() -> datetime:
    return datetime(2026, 1, 1, 12, 0, tzinfo=UTC)


def _metadata() -> ProjectMetadata:
    return ProjectMetadata(
        project_id=ProjectId("p-001"),
        project_name="Case 001",
        patient_id="pat-001",
        patient_display_name="Gabriel Silva",
        clinical_procedure="orthognathic",
        status=ProjectStatus.ACTIVE,
        created_at=_now(),
        updated_at=_now(),
        last_opened_at=None,
        schema_version=1,
    )


def _summary(**overrides) -> ProjectSummary:
    base: dict = {
        "project_id": ProjectId("p-001"),
        "project_name": "Case 001",
        "patient_display_name": "Gabriel Silva",
        "status": ProjectStatus.ACTIVE,
        "procedure": "orthognathic",
        "created_at": _now(),
        "updated_at": _now(),
        "last_opened_at": None,
    }
    base.update(overrides)
    return ProjectSummary(**base)


# ----- Construction -------------------------------------------------


def test_summary_holds_all_fields() -> None:
    s = _summary()
    assert s.project_id == ProjectId("p-001")
    assert s.project_name == "Case 001"
    assert s.patient_display_name == "Gabriel Silva"
    assert s.status is ProjectStatus.ACTIVE
    assert s.procedure == "orthognathic"


def test_summary_is_frozen() -> None:
    s = _summary()
    attr = "project_name"
    with pytest.raises(dataclasses.FrozenInstanceError):
        setattr(s, attr, "Other")


def test_empty_project_name_raises() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="project_name"):
        _summary(project_name="")


def test_empty_patient_display_name_raises() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="patient_display_name"):
        _summary(patient_display_name="")


def test_empty_procedure_raises() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="procedure"):
        _summary(procedure="")


# ----- from_metadata ------------------------------------------------


def test_from_metadata_copies_fields() -> None:
    md = _metadata()
    s = ProjectSummary.from_metadata(md)

    assert s.project_id == md.project_id
    assert s.project_name == md.project_name
    assert s.patient_display_name == md.patient_display_name
    assert s.status == md.status
    assert s.procedure == md.clinical_procedure
    assert s.created_at == md.created_at
    assert s.updated_at == md.updated_at
    assert s.last_opened_at == md.last_opened_at


def test_from_metadata_with_last_opened() -> None:
    later = datetime(2026, 2, 1, tzinfo=UTC)
    md = _metadata().with_last_opened(now=later)
    s = ProjectSummary.from_metadata(md)
    assert s.last_opened_at == later

======================================================================
FILE: tests/unit/infrastructure/app/test_app_path_resolver.py
======================================================================

"""Tests for AppPathResolver."""

from __future__ import annotations

from pathlib import Path

import pytest

from cranioz.config.schema import PathsConfig
from cranioz.infrastructure.app.app_path_resolver import (
    AppPathInaccessible,
    AppPathResolver,
)

# ----- Helpers ------------------------------------------------------


def _paths_config(
    *,
    project_root: str | None = None,
    flows_root: str | None = None,
    cache_root: str | None = None,
) -> PathsConfig:
    return PathsConfig(
        project_root=project_root,
        flows_root=flows_root,
        cache_root=cache_root,
    )


def _initialize(
    tmp_path: Path,
    *,
    project_root: str | None = None,
    flows_root: str | None = None,
    cache_root: str | None = None,
) -> AppPathResolver:
    """Build a resolver with all roots pointed at tmp_path subdirs.

    Any root not overridden gets a tmp_path subdirectory, so tests
    never touch the user's real home directory.
    """
    cfg = _paths_config(
        project_root=project_root or str(tmp_path / "projects"),
        flows_root=flows_root or str(tmp_path / "flows"),
        cache_root=cache_root or str(tmp_path / "cache"),
    )
    resolver = AppPathResolver()
    resolver.initialize(cfg)
    return resolver


# ----- Initialization -----------------------------------------------


def test_initialize_creates_directories(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    resolver = _initialize(tmp_path)

    assert resolver.projects_root.is_dir()
    assert resolver.flows_root.is_dir()
    assert resolver.cache_root.is_dir()


def test_initialize_uses_configured_paths(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    projects = tmp_path / "my_projects"
    flows = tmp_path / "my_flows"
    cache = tmp_path / "my_cache"

    resolver = AppPathResolver()
    resolver.initialize(
        _paths_config(
            project_root=str(projects),
            flows_root=str(flows),
            cache_root=str(cache),
        )
    )

    assert resolver.projects_root == projects
    assert resolver.flows_root == flows
    assert resolver.cache_root == cache


def test_initialize_uses_defaults_when_none(
    tmp_path: Path, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch
) -> None:
    monkeypatch.setattr(Path, "home", classmethod(lambda cls: tmp_path))

    resolver = AppPathResolver()
    resolver.initialize(_paths_config())

    assert resolver.projects_root == (tmp_path / "Documents" / "CranioZ" / "Projects")
    assert resolver.flows_root == (tmp_path / "Documents" / "CranioZ" / "Flows")
    assert resolver.cache_root == tmp_path / ".cranioz" / "Cache"


def test_initialize_without_create_fails_if_missing(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    missing = tmp_path / "does_not_exist"

    resolver = AppPathResolver()
    with pytest.raises(AppPathInaccessible, match="does not exist"):
        resolver.initialize(
            _paths_config(
                project_root=str(missing),
                flows_root=str(tmp_path / "flows"),
                cache_root=str(tmp_path / "cache"),
            ),
            create=False,
        )


def test_initialize_fails_if_path_is_a_file(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    file_path = tmp_path / "not_a_dir"
    file_path.write_text("x", encoding="utf-8")

    resolver = AppPathResolver()
    with pytest.raises(AppPathInaccessible, match="not a directory"):
        resolver.initialize(
            _paths_config(
                project_root=str(file_path),
                flows_root=str(tmp_path / "flows"),
                cache_root=str(tmp_path / "cache"),
            ),
            create=False,
        )


# ----- Accessors before initialization ------------------------------


def test_projects_root_before_initialize_raises() -> None:
    resolver = AppPathResolver()
    with pytest.raises(AppPathInaccessible, match="not initialized"):
        _ = resolver.projects_root


def test_flows_root_before_initialize_raises() -> None:
    resolver = AppPathResolver()
    with pytest.raises(AppPathInaccessible, match="not initialized"):
        _ = resolver.flows_root


def test_cache_root_before_initialize_raises() -> None:
    resolver = AppPathResolver()
    with pytest.raises(AppPathInaccessible, match="not initialized"):
        _ = resolver.cache_root


# ----- resolve_in_projects_root -------------------------------------


def test_resolve_in_projects_root(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    resolver = _initialize(tmp_path)
    resolved = resolver.resolve_in_projects_root("abc/imaging/ct")
    assert resolved == (resolver.projects_root / "abc" / "imaging" / "ct").resolve()


def test_resolve_in_projects_root_rejects_traversal(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    resolver = _initialize(tmp_path)
    with pytest.raises(AppPathInaccessible, match="escapes"):
        resolver.resolve_in_projects_root("../outside")


def test_resolve_in_projects_root_rejects_absolute_escape(
    tmp_path: Path,
) -> None:
    resolver = _initialize(tmp_path)
    with pytest.raises(AppPathInaccessible, match="escapes"):
        resolver.resolve_in_projects_root("../../../etc/passwd")


def test_resolve_in_projects_root_before_initialize_raises() -> None:
    resolver = AppPathResolver()
    with pytest.raises(AppPathInaccessible, match="not initialized"):
        resolver.resolve_in_projects_root("anything")



======================================================================
FILE: tests/unit/infrastructure/project/test_project_path_resolver.py
======================================================================

"""Tests for ProjectPathResolver.

ProjectPathResolver derives every file path inside a CranioZ
project from a ProjectId and the projects root. It never escapes
the project directory, and it validates the ProjectId at
construction time.

These tests do not touch the real filesystem except in the
``exists()`` tests, which use ``tmp_path``.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from pathlib import Path

import pytest

from cranioz.domain.project.project_id import ProjectId
from cranioz.infrastructure.project.project_path_resolver import (
    PATIENT_RECORD_FILE_NAME,
    PROJECT_FILE_NAME,
    WORKFLOW_FILE_NAME,
    ProjectPathInaccessible,
    ProjectPathResolver,
)

# ----- Helpers ------------------------------------------------------


def _resolver(
    projects_root: Path,
    project_id: str = "abc-123",
) -> ProjectPathResolver:
    return ProjectPathResolver(
        projects_root=projects_root,
        project_id=ProjectId(project_id),
    )


# ----- Identity -----------------------------------------------------


def test_resolver_holds_projects_root(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    resolver = _resolver(tmp_path)
    assert resolver.projects_root == tmp_path


def test_resolver_holds_project_id(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    resolver = _resolver(tmp_path, "abc-123")
    assert resolver.project_id == ProjectId("abc-123")


# ----- Standard paths -----------------------------------------------


def test_project_dir_is_under_projects_root(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    resolver = _resolver(tmp_path, "abc-123")
    assert resolver.project_dir == tmp_path / "abc-123"


def test_project_file(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    resolver = _resolver(tmp_path, "abc-123")
    assert resolver.project_file == tmp_path / "abc-123" / PROJECT_FILE_NAME


def test_patient_record_file(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    resolver = _resolver(tmp_path, "abc-123")
    assert resolver.patient_record_file == (tmp_path / "abc-123" / PATIENT_RECORD_FILE_NAME)


def test_workflow_file(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    resolver = _resolver(tmp_path, "abc-123")
    assert resolver.workflow_file == tmp_path / "abc-123" / WORKFLOW_FILE_NAME


def test_standard_file_names_are_constants() -> None:
    assert PROJECT_FILE_NAME == "project.json"
    assert PATIENT_RECORD_FILE_NAME == "patient_record.json"
    assert WORKFLOW_FILE_NAME == "workflow.json"


# ----- resource() ---------------------------------------------------


def test_resource_with_posix_separator(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    resolver = _resolver(tmp_path, "abc-123")
    resolved = resolver.resource("imaging/ct.dcm")
    assert resolved == (tmp_path / "abc-123" / "imaging" / "ct.dcm").resolve()


def test_resource_with_nested_path(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    resolver = _resolver(tmp_path, "abc-123")
    resolved = resolver.resource("planning/osteotomies/lefort_i.json")
    assert (
        resolved == (tmp_path / "abc-123" / "planning" / "osteotomies" / "lefort_i.json").resolve()
    )


def test_resource_rejects_empty(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    resolver = _resolver(tmp_path)
    with pytest.raises(ProjectPathInaccessible, match="must not be empty"):
        resolver.resource("")


def test_resource_rejects_absolute_posix(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    resolver = _resolver(tmp_path)
    with pytest.raises(ProjectPathInaccessible, match="absolute"):
        resolver.resource("/etc/passwd")


def test_resource_rejects_traversal(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    resolver = _resolver(tmp_path)
    with pytest.raises(ProjectPathInaccessible, match="escapes"):
        resolver.resource("../outside")


def test_resource_rejects_deep_traversal(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    resolver = _resolver(tmp_path)
    with pytest.raises(ProjectPathInaccessible, match="escapes"):
        resolver.resource("../../etc/passwd")


def test_resource_accepts_path_that_stays_inside(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    # "foo/../bar" resolves back inside the project. The final
    # resolved path is inside, so it is accepted.
    resolver = _resolver(tmp_path, "abc-123")
    resolved = resolver.resource("foo/../bar")
    assert resolved == (tmp_path / "abc-123" / "bar").resolve()


# ----- ProjectId validation -----------------------------------------


def test_project_id_empty_is_rejected(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    # ProjectId itself rejects empty; we cannot even build it.
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        ProjectId("")


def test_project_id_with_slash_is_rejected(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ProjectPathInaccessible, match="separators"):
        ProjectPathResolver(
            projects_root=tmp_path,
            project_id=ProjectId("abc/def"),
        )


def test_project_id_with_backslash_is_rejected(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ProjectPathInaccessible, match="separators"):
        ProjectPathResolver(
            projects_root=tmp_path,
            project_id=ProjectId("abc\\def"),
        )


def test_project_id_with_dotdot_is_rejected(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ProjectPathInaccessible, match=r"\.\."):
        ProjectPathResolver(
            projects_root=tmp_path,
            project_id=ProjectId("abc..def"),
        )


def test_project_id_with_colon_is_rejected(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ProjectPathInaccessible, match=":"):
        ProjectPathResolver(
            projects_root=tmp_path,
            project_id=ProjectId("C:abc"),
        )


def test_project_id_starting_with_dot_is_rejected(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ProjectPathInaccessible, match="start with"):
        ProjectPathResolver(
            projects_root=tmp_path,
            project_id=ProjectId(".hidden"),
        )


def test_project_id_with_control_character_is_rejected(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ProjectPathInaccessible, match="control"):
        ProjectPathResolver(
            projects_root=tmp_path,
            project_id=ProjectId("abc\x00def"),
        )


def test_project_id_with_newline_is_rejected(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ProjectPathInaccessible, match="control"):
        ProjectPathResolver(
            projects_root=tmp_path,
            project_id=ProjectId("abc\ndef"),
        )


# ----- exists() -----------------------------------------------------


def test_exists_returns_false_when_dir_missing(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    resolver = _resolver(tmp_path, "abc-123")
    assert resolver.exists() is False


def test_exists_returns_true_when_dir_present(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    project_dir = tmp_path / "abc-123"
    project_dir.mkdir()
    resolver = _resolver(tmp_path, "abc-123")
    assert resolver.exists() is True


def test_exists_is_false_when_path_is_a_file(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    # A file where the project directory should be is not "existing".
    file_path = tmp_path / "abc-123"
    file_path.write_text("x", encoding="utf-8")
    resolver = _resolver(tmp_path, "abc-123")
    assert resolver.exists() is False

FILE: tests/unit/ui/editors/test_base_editor.py
======================================================================

"""Tests for the Editor ABC."""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any

import pytest

from cranioz.ui.editors.base_editor import Editor
from cranioz.ui.editors.editor_context import EditorContext


class ConcreteEditor(Editor):
    """Minimal concrete Editor used only in tests."""

    def __init__(self, editor_id: str = "editor.test", title: str = "Test") -> None:
        super().__init__(editor_id=editor_id, title=title)
        self.initialize_calls = 0
        self.activate_calls = 0
        self.deactivate_calls = 0
        self.dispose_calls = 0
        self._state: dict[str, Any] = {}

    def content_widget(self) -> str:
        return f"<{self.editor_id}>"

    def initialize(self, context: EditorContext) -> None:
        self.initialize_calls += 1

    def activate(self) -> None:
        self.activate_calls += 1

    def deactivate(self) -> None:
        self.deactivate_calls += 1

    def dispose(self) -> None:
        self.dispose_calls += 1

    def persistent_state(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return dict(self._state)

    def restore_state(self, state: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
        self._state = dict(state)


def test_editor_is_abstract() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(TypeError):
        Editor(editor_id="x", title="X")  # type: ignore[abstract]


def test_editor_stores_id_and_title() -> None:
    editor = ConcreteEditor(editor_id="editor.a", title="Alpha")
    assert editor.editor_id == "editor.a"
    assert editor.title == "Alpha"


def test_content_widget_returns_expected_value() -> None:
    editor = ConcreteEditor(editor_id="editor.a")
    assert editor.content_widget() == "<editor.a>"


def test_default_toolbar_specs_is_empty() -> None:
    editor = ConcreteEditor()
    assert editor.toolbar_specs() == []


def test_default_persistent_state_is_empty() -> None:
    # The base class returns {} by default.
    # Our ConcreteEditor returns its own state, so test the base directly.
    class Minimal(Editor):
        def content_widget(self) -> str:
            return ""

    minimal = Minimal(editor_id="editor.min", title="Min")
    assert minimal.persistent_state() == {}


def test_restore_state_round_trip() -> None:
    editor = ConcreteEditor()
    editor.restore_state({"zoom": 2.0})
    assert editor.persistent_state() == {"zoom": 2.0}


def test_lifecycle_methods_can_be_called() -> None:
    editor = ConcreteEditor()
    ctx = EditorContext(editor_id="editor.test")
    editor.initialize(ctx)
    editor.activate()
    editor.deactivate()
    editor.dispose()
    assert editor.initialize_calls == 1
    assert editor.activate_calls == 1
    assert editor.deactivate_calls == 1
    assert editor.dispose_calls == 1

FILE: tests/unit/ui/editors/test_editor_context.py
======================================================================

"""Tests for EditorContext."""

from __future__ import annotations

import dataclasses

import pytest

from cranioz.ui.editors.editor_context import EditorContext


def test_editor_context_holds_editor_id() -> None:
    ctx = EditorContext(editor_id="editor.a")
    assert ctx.editor_id == "editor.a"
    assert ctx.extras == {}


def test_editor_context_holds_extras() -> None:
    ctx = EditorContext(editor_id="editor.a", extras={"zoom": 1.5})
    assert ctx.extras["zoom"] == 1.5


def test_editor_context_is_frozen() -> None:
    ctx = EditorContext(editor_id="editor.a")
    with pytest.raises(dataclasses.FrozenInstanceError):
        ctx.editor_id = "editor.b"  # type: ignore[misc]


def test_two_editor_contexts_with_same_data_are_equal() -> None:
    a = EditorContext(editor_id="editor.a")
    b = EditorContext(editor_id="editor.a")
    assert a == b


def test_default_extras_are_not_shared_between_instances() -> None:
    a = EditorContext(editor_id="editor.a")
    b = EditorContext(editor_id="editor.b")
    # dicts created via default_factory are independent
    assert a.extras is not b.extras

FILE: tests/unit/ui/editors/test_editor_registry.py
======================================================================

"""Tests for EditorRegistry."""

# mypy: ignore-errors
# This file exercises Qt widgets and the Editor contract with type-ignore needs.

from __future__ import annotations

import pytest
from pytestqt.qtbot import QtBot

from cranioz.ui.editors.base_editor import Editor
from cranioz.ui.editors.builtin.test_editor import DummyEditor
from cranioz.ui.editors.editor_registry import (
    EditorRegistry,
    EditorRegistryError,
)


class OtherEditor(Editor):
    """A second concrete Editor for tests."""

    def content_widget(self) -> str:
        return self.editor_id


# ----- Registration ------------------------------------------------


def test_register_and_resolve() -> None:
    registry = EditorRegistry()
    registry.register("editor.test", DummyEditor)
    assert registry.resolve("editor.test") is DummyEditor


def test_register_idempotent_for_same_class() -> None:
    registry = EditorRegistry()
    registry.register("editor.test", DummyEditor)
    registry.register("editor.test", DummyEditor)  # no raise
    assert registry.contains("editor.test")


def test_register_different_class_for_same_id_raises() -> None:
    registry = EditorRegistry()
    registry.register("editor.test", DummyEditor)
    with pytest.raises(EditorRegistryError, match="different type"):
        registry.register("editor.test", OtherEditor)


def test_register_empty_id_raises() -> None:
    registry = EditorRegistry()
    with pytest.raises(EditorRegistryError, match="must not be empty"):
        registry.register("", DummyEditor)


# ----- Resolution and creation -------------------------------------


def test_resolve_unknown_raises_keyerror() -> None:
    registry = EditorRegistry()
    with pytest.raises(KeyError, match="not registered"):
        registry.resolve("editor.unknown")


def test_create_returns_new_instance(qtbot: QtBot) -> None:
    registry = EditorRegistry()
    registry.register("editor.test", DummyEditor)

    editor = registry.create("editor.test", editor_id="editor.a", title="Alpha")
    widget = editor.content_widget()
    qtbot.addWidget(widget)

    assert isinstance(editor, DummyEditor)
    assert editor.editor_id == "editor.a"
    assert editor.title == "Alpha"


def test_create_twice_returns_distinct_instances(qtbot: QtBot) -> None:
    registry = EditorRegistry()
    registry.register("editor.test", DummyEditor)

    a = registry.create("editor.test", editor_id="editor.a")
    b = registry.create("editor.test", editor_id="editor.b")
    qtbot.addWidget(a.content_widget())
    qtbot.addWidget(b.content_widget())

    assert a is not b


def test_create_unknown_raises_keyerror() -> None:
    registry = EditorRegistry()
    with pytest.raises(KeyError, match="not registered"):
        registry.create("editor.unknown")


# ----- Unregister and inspect --------------------------------------


def test_unregister_removes() -> None:
    registry = EditorRegistry()
    registry.register("editor.test", DummyEditor)
    registry.unregister("editor.test")
    assert not registry.contains("editor.test")


def test_unregister_unknown_is_noop() -> None:
    registry = EditorRegistry()
    registry.unregister("editor.unknown")  # no raise


def test_identifiers_in_registration_order() -> None:
    registry = EditorRegistry()
    registry.register("editor.b", DummyEditor)
    registry.register("editor.a", DummyEditor)
    assert registry.identifiers() == ("editor.b", "editor.a")


def test_contains() -> None:
    registry = EditorRegistry()
    assert not registry.contains("editor.test")
    registry.register("editor.test", DummyEditor)
    assert registry.contains("editor.test")

"""Tests for TestEditor."""

# mypy: ignore-errors
# This file exercises the Editor contract with Qt widgets.

from __future__ import annotations

from PySide6.QtWidgets import QLabel
from pytestqt.qtbot import QtBot

from cranioz.ui.editors.builtin.test_editor import DummyEditor
from cranioz.ui.editors.editor_context import EditorContext
from cranioz.ui.toolbars.specification import ToolbarOrientation

# ----- Metadata ----------------------------------------------------


def test_editor_metadata() -> None:
    editor = DummyEditor(editor_id="editor.a", title="Alpha")
    assert editor.editor_id == "editor.a"
    assert editor.title == "Alpha"


def test_default_editor_id_and_title() -> None:
    editor = DummyEditor()
    assert editor.editor_id == "editor.test"
    assert editor.title == "Test Editor"


# ----- Content widget ----------------------------------------------


def test_content_widget_is_qlabel(qtbot: QtBot) -> None:
    editor = DummyEditor(editor_id="editor.a")
    widget = editor.content_widget()
    qtbot.addWidget(widget)
    assert isinstance(widget, QLabel)


def test_content_widget_shows_editor_id(qtbot: QtBot) -> None:
    editor = DummyEditor(editor_id="editor.a")
    widget = editor.content_widget()
    qtbot.addWidget(widget)
    assert widget.text() == "editor.a"


def test_content_widget_is_cached(qtbot: QtBot) -> None:
    editor = DummyEditor(editor_id="editor.a")
    widget1 = editor.content_widget()
    widget2 = editor.content_widget()
    qtbot.addWidget(widget1)
    assert widget1 is widget2


def test_content_widget_has_object_name(qtbot: QtBot) -> None:
    editor = DummyEditor(editor_id="editor.a")
    widget = editor.content_widget()
    qtbot.addWidget(widget)
    assert widget.objectName() == "dummy_editor.content.editor.a"


# ----- Toolbar specs ------------------------------------------------


def test_toolbar_specs_returns_one_spec() -> None:
    editor = DummyEditor(editor_id="editor.a")
    specs = editor.toolbar_specs()
    assert len(specs) == 1


def test_toolbar_spec_has_expected_id() -> None:
    editor = DummyEditor(editor_id="editor.a")
    spec = editor.toolbar_specs()[0]
    assert spec.toolbar_id == "toolbar.editor.a"


def test_toolbar_spec_has_log_and_toggle_tools() -> None:
    editor = DummyEditor(editor_id="editor.a")
    spec = editor.toolbar_specs()[0]
    assert "tool.log" in spec.tool_ids
    assert "tool.toggle" in spec.tool_ids


def test_toolbar_spec_respects_orientation() -> None:
    editor = DummyEditor(
        editor_id="editor.a",
        toolbar_orientation=ToolbarOrientation.VERTICAL,
    )
    spec = editor.toolbar_specs()[0]
    assert spec.orientation is ToolbarOrientation.VERTICAL


# ----- Persistence -------------------------------------------------


def test_persistent_state_before_initialize_is_empty() -> None:
    editor = DummyEditor(editor_id="editor.a")
    assert editor.persistent_state() == {}


def test_persistent_state_after_initialize() -> None:
    editor = DummyEditor(editor_id="editor.a")
    ctx = EditorContext(editor_id="editor.a", extras={"zoom": 2.0})
    editor.initialize(ctx)
    state = editor.persistent_state()
    assert state["editor_id"] == "editor.a"
    assert state["extras"] == {"zoom": 2.0}


def test_restore_state_is_noop() -> None:
    editor = DummyEditor(editor_id="editor.a")
    editor.restore_state({"any": "thing"})


# ----- Lifecycle ---------------------------------------------------


def test_initialize_stores_context() -> None:
    editor = DummyEditor(editor_id="editor.a")
    ctx = EditorContext(editor_id="editor.a")
    editor.initialize(ctx)
    assert editor.persistent_state()["editor_id"] == "editor.a"

"""Tests for ThemeService."""

from __future__ import annotations

import dataclasses

import pytest

from cranioz.ui.theme.service import ThemeName, ThemeService


def test_default_theme_is_dark() -> None:
    service = ThemeService()
    assert service.theme is ThemeName.DARK


def test_with_theme_returns_new_instance() -> None:
    service = ThemeService()
    light = service.with_theme(ThemeName.LIGHT)
    assert light is not service
    assert light.theme is ThemeName.LIGHT
    assert service.theme is ThemeName.DARK  # original unchanged


def test_with_theme_is_idempotent_for_same_theme() -> None:
    service = ThemeService(theme=ThemeName.LIGHT)
    again = service.with_theme(ThemeName.LIGHT)
    assert again == service
    assert again is not service


def test_icon_name_is_identity() -> None:
    service = ThemeService()
    assert service.icon_name("log") == "log"
    assert service.icon_name("toggle") == "toggle"


def test_icon_name_is_theme_agnostic() -> None:
    dark = ThemeService(theme=ThemeName.DARK)
    light = ThemeService(theme=ThemeName.LIGHT)
    assert dark.icon_name("x") == light.icon_name("x")


def test_empty_icon_name_raises() -> None:
    service = ThemeService()
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="logical_name"):
        service.icon_name("")


def test_theme_name_members() -> None:
    expected = {"LIGHT", "DARK"}
    actual = {member.name for member in ThemeName}
    assert actual == expected


def test_theme_service_is_frozen() -> None:
    service = ThemeService()
    with pytest.raises(dataclasses.FrozenInstanceError):
        service.theme = ThemeName.LIGHT  # type: ignore[misc]

"""Tests for ToolbarItem types."""

# mypy: ignore-errors
# This file intentionally mutates frozen dataclasses to test immutability.

from __future__ import annotations

import dataclasses

import pytest

from cranioz.ui.toolbars.item import (
    ActionItem,
    Separator,
    ToggleItem,
    ToolbarItemKind,
)

# ----- ActionItem --------------------------------------------------


def test_action_item_holds_fields() -> None:
    item = ActionItem(tool_id="tool.log", label="Log")
    assert item.tool_id == "tool.log"
    assert item.label == "Log"
    assert item.icon is None
    assert item.kind is ToolbarItemKind.ACTION


def test_action_item_with_icon() -> None:
    item = ActionItem(tool_id="tool.log", label="Log", icon="log")
    assert item.icon == "log"


def test_action_item_requires_tool_id() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="tool_id"):
        ActionItem(tool_id="", label="Log")


def test_action_item_requires_label() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="label"):
        ActionItem(tool_id="tool.log", label="")


# ----- ToggleItem --------------------------------------------------


def test_toggle_item_defaults() -> None:
    item = ToggleItem(tool_id="tool.grid", label="Grid")
    assert item.tool_id == "tool.grid"
    assert item.label == "Grid"
    assert item.icon is None
    assert item.checked is False
    assert item.kind is ToolbarItemKind.TOGGLE


def test_toggle_item_checked_true() -> None:
    item = ToggleItem(tool_id="tool.grid", label="Grid", checked=True)
    assert item.checked is True


def test_toggle_item_requires_tool_id() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="tool_id"):
        ToggleItem(tool_id="", label="Grid")


def test_toggle_item_requires_label() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="label"):
        ToggleItem(tool_id="tool.grid", label="")


# ----- Separator ---------------------------------------------------


def test_separator_has_separator_kind() -> None:
    sep = Separator()
    assert sep.kind is ToolbarItemKind.SEPARATOR


def test_separators_are_equal() -> None:
    assert Separator() == Separator()


# ----- Equality and immutability -----------------------------------


def test_action_items_with_same_data_are_equal() -> None:
    a = ActionItem(tool_id="tool.log", label="Log")
    b = ActionItem(tool_id="tool.log", label="Log")
    assert a == b


def test_action_items_with_different_data_are_not_equal() -> None:
    a = ActionItem(tool_id="tool.log", label="Log")
    b = ActionItem(tool_id="tool.other", label="Other")
    assert a != b


def test_action_item_is_frozen() -> None:
    item = ActionItem(tool_id="tool.log", label="Log")
    with pytest.raises(dataclasses.FrozenInstanceError):
        item.label = "Other"


def test_toggle_item_is_frozen() -> None:
    item = ToggleItem(tool_id="tool.grid", label="Grid")
    with pytest.raises(dataclasses.FrozenInstanceError):
        item.checked = True


# ----- Enum --------------------------------------------------------


def test_toolbar_item_kind_members() -> None:
    expected = {"ACTION", "TOGGLE", "SEPARATOR"}
    actual = {member.name for member in ToolbarItemKind}
    assert actual == expected

"""Tests for ToolbarState."""

from __future__ import annotations

import pytest

from cranioz.ui.toolbars.specification import ToolbarVisibilityMode
from cranioz.ui.toolbars.state import ToolbarState, ToolbarStateError


def test_toolbar_state_defaults() -> None:
    state = ToolbarState(toolbar_id="tb.x")
    assert state.toolbar_id == "tb.x"
    assert state.visible is True


def test_initial_for_always_is_visible() -> None:
    state = ToolbarState.initial_for("tb.x", ToolbarVisibilityMode.ALWAYS)
    assert state.visible is True


def test_initial_for_collapsible_is_visible() -> None:
    state = ToolbarState.initial_for("tb.x", ToolbarVisibilityMode.COLLAPSIBLE)
    assert state.visible is True


def test_initial_for_auto_hide_is_hidden() -> None:
    state = ToolbarState.initial_for("tb.x", ToolbarVisibilityMode.AUTO_HIDE)
    assert state.visible is False


def test_initial_for_hidden_is_hidden() -> None:
    state = ToolbarState.initial_for("tb.x", ToolbarVisibilityMode.HIDDEN)
    assert state.visible is False


def test_initial_for_unknown_mode_raises() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ToolbarStateError, match="unknown visibility mode"):
        ToolbarState.initial_for("tb.x", "not-a-mode")  # type: ignore[arg-type]


def test_state_is_mutable() -> None:
    state = ToolbarState(toolbar_id="tb.x", visible=True)
    state.visible = False
    assert state.visible is False

FILE: tests/unit/ui/toolbars/test_toolbar.py

"""Tests for the Toolbar widget.

These tests validate that the Toolbar materializes a ToolbarSpec
against a ToolRegistry and a ThemeService, and that user interaction
produces the expected signals.
"""

# mypy: ignore-errors
# This file exercises Qt widgets and intentionally triggers edge cases.

from __future__ import annotations

from PySide6.QtWidgets import QPushButton
from pytestqt.qtbot import QtBot

from cranioz.ui.theme.service import ThemeService
from cranioz.ui.toolbars.base import Toolbar
from cranioz.ui.toolbars.specification import ToolbarOrientation
from cranioz.ui.tools.registry.tool_definition import ToolContext
from cranioz.ui.tools.registry.tool_registry import ToolRegistry

# ----- Construction --------------------------------------------------


def test_empty_toolbar_has_no_bindings(
    qtbot: QtBot,
    tool_registry: ToolRegistry,
    theme_service: ThemeService,
    make_toolbar_spec,
) -> None:
    spec = make_toolbar_spec(tool_ids=())
    toolbar = Toolbar(spec, tool_registry, theme_service)
    qtbot.addWidget(toolbar)

    assert toolbar.bindings() == ()
    assert toolbar.tool_ids() == ()


def test_toolbar_with_one_tool_creates_one_binding(
    qtbot: QtBot,
    tool_registry: ToolRegistry,
    theme_service: ThemeService,
    make_toolbar_spec,
) -> None:
    spec = make_toolbar_spec(tool_ids=("tool.log",))
    toolbar = Toolbar(spec, tool_registry, theme_service)
    qtbot.addWidget(toolbar)

    assert toolbar.tool_ids() == ("tool.log",)


def test_toolbar_with_two_tools_preserves_order(
    qtbot: QtBot,
    tool_registry: ToolRegistry,
    theme_service: ThemeService,
    make_toolbar_spec,
) -> None:
    spec = make_toolbar_spec(tool_ids=("tool.toggle", "tool.log"))
    toolbar = Toolbar(spec, tool_registry, theme_service)
    qtbot.addWidget(toolbar)

    assert toolbar.tool_ids() == ("tool.toggle", "tool.log")


def test_toolbar_resolves_tool_instances(
    qtbot: QtBot,
    tool_registry: ToolRegistry,
    theme_service: ThemeService,
    make_toolbar_spec,
) -> None:
    spec = make_toolbar_spec(tool_ids=("tool.log",))
    toolbar = Toolbar(spec, tool_registry, theme_service)
    qtbot.addWidget(toolbar)

    binding = toolbar.bindings()[0]
    assert binding.tool is not None
    assert binding.tool.identifier == "tool.log"


# ----- Orientation ---------------------------------------------------


def test_horizontal_toolbar_renders_buttons(
    qtbot: QtBot,
    tool_registry: ToolRegistry,
    theme_service: ThemeService,
    make_toolbar_spec,
) -> None:
    spec = make_toolbar_spec(
        orientation=ToolbarOrientation.HORIZONTAL,
        tool_ids=("tool.log",),
    )
    toolbar = Toolbar(spec, tool_registry, theme_service)
    qtbot.addWidget(toolbar)

    buttons = toolbar.findChildren(QPushButton)
    assert len(buttons) == 1
    assert buttons[0].text() == "Log"


def test_vertical_toolbar_renders_buttons(
    qtbot: QtBot,
    tool_registry: ToolRegistry,
    theme_service: ThemeService,
    make_toolbar_spec,
) -> None:
    spec = make_toolbar_spec(
        orientation=ToolbarOrientation.VERTICAL,
        tool_ids=("tool.toggle",),
    )
    toolbar = Toolbar(spec, tool_registry, theme_service)
    qtbot.addWidget(toolbar)

    buttons = toolbar.findChildren(QPushButton)
    assert len(buttons) == 1
    assert buttons[0].text() == "Toggle"


# ----- Icon resolution ----------------------------------------------


def test_toolbar_resolves_icon_name_via_theme(
    qtbot: QtBot,
    tool_registry: ToolRegistry,
    theme_service: ThemeService,
    make_toolbar_spec,
) -> None:
    spec = make_toolbar_spec(tool_ids=("tool.log",))
    toolbar = Toolbar(spec, tool_registry, theme_service)
    qtbot.addWidget(toolbar)

    button = toolbar.findChild(QPushButton)
    assert button is not None
    # The button stores the resolved icon name as a dynamic property.
    # The actual icon file is not loaded in this phase.
    assert button.property("icon_name") == "log"


# ----- Signals ------------------------------------------------------


def test_clicking_action_tool_emits_signal(
    qtbot: QtBot,
    tool_registry: ToolRegistry,
    theme_service: ThemeService,
    make_toolbar_spec,
) -> None:
    spec = make_toolbar_spec(tool_ids=("tool.log",))
    toolbar = Toolbar(spec, tool_registry, theme_service)
    qtbot.addWidget(toolbar)

    with qtbot.waitSignal(toolbar.tool_triggered, timeout=1000) as blocker:
        button = toolbar.findChild(QPushButton)
        assert button is not None
        button.click()

    assert blocker.args == ["tool.log"]


def test_toggling_toggle_tool_emits_signal(
    qtbot: QtBot,
    tool_registry: ToolRegistry,
    theme_service: ThemeService,
    make_toolbar_spec,
) -> None:
    spec = make_toolbar_spec(tool_ids=("tool.toggle",))
    toolbar = Toolbar(spec, tool_registry, theme_service)
    qtbot.addWidget(toolbar)

    with qtbot.waitSignal(toolbar.tool_toggled, timeout=1000) as blocker:
        button = toolbar.findChild(QPushButton)
        assert button is not None
        assert button.isCheckable()
        button.click()

    tool_id, checked = blocker.args
    assert tool_id == "tool.toggle"
    assert checked is True


# ----- Interaction guards -------------------------------------------


def test_disabled_tool_does_not_emit_signal(
    qtbot: QtBot,
    tool_registry: ToolRegistry,
    theme_service: ThemeService,
    make_toolbar_spec,
) -> None:
    spec = make_toolbar_spec(tool_ids=("tool.log",))
    ctx = ToolContext(extras={"enabled": False})
    toolbar = Toolbar(spec, tool_registry, theme_service, context=ctx)
    qtbot.addWidget(toolbar)

    emitted: list[str] = []
    toolbar.tool_triggered.connect(emitted.append)

    button = toolbar.findChild(QPushButton)
    assert button is not None
    button.click()

    assert emitted == []


def test_unavailable_tool_does_not_emit_signal(
    qtbot: QtBot,
    tool_registry: ToolRegistry,
    theme_service: ThemeService,
    make_toolbar_spec,
) -> None:
    spec = make_toolbar_spec(tool_ids=("tool.log",))
    ctx = ToolContext(extras={"available": False})
    toolbar = Toolbar(spec, tool_registry, theme_service, context=ctx)
    qtbot.addWidget(toolbar)

    emitted: list[str] = []
    toolbar.tool_triggered.connect(emitted.append)

    button = toolbar.findChild(QPushButton)
    assert button is not None
    button.click()

    assert emitted == []


# ----- Errors -------------------------------------------------------


def test_unknown_tool_id_raises(
    qtbot: QtBot,
    tool_registry: ToolRegistry,
    theme_service: ThemeService,
    make_toolbar_spec,
) -> None:
    spec = make_toolbar_spec(tool_ids=("tool.unknown",))
    try:
        toolbar = Toolbar(spec, tool_registry, theme_service)
    except KeyError as exc:
        assert "tool.unknown" in str(exc)
    else:
        qtbot.addWidget(toolbar)
        raise AssertionError("expected KeyError for unknown tool_id")
