"""Tests for ToolbarSpec."""

from __future__ import annotations

import pytest

from cranioz.ui.toolbars.specification import (
    ToolbarOrientation,
    ToolbarSpec,
    ToolbarSpecError,
    ToolbarVisibilityMode,
)


def test_toolbar_spec_defaults() -> None:
    spec = ToolbarSpec(toolbar_id="tb.x", title="X")
    assert spec.toolbar_id == "tb.x"
    assert spec.title == "X"
    assert spec.orientation is ToolbarOrientation.HORIZONTAL
    assert spec.tool_ids == ()
    assert spec.visibility_mode is ToolbarVisibilityMode.ALWAYS


def test_toolbar_spec_with_all_fields() -> None:
    spec = ToolbarSpec(
        toolbar_id="tb.y",
        title="Y",
        orientation=ToolbarOrientation.VERTICAL,
        tool_ids=("tool.a", "tool.b"),
        visibility_mode=ToolbarVisibilityMode.AUTO_HIDE,
    )
    assert spec.orientation is ToolbarOrientation.VERTICAL
    assert spec.tool_ids == ("tool.a", "tool.b")
    assert spec.visibility_mode is ToolbarVisibilityMode.AUTO_HIDE


def test_empty_toolbar_id_raises() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ToolbarSpecError, match="toolbar_id"):
        ToolbarSpec(toolbar_id="", title="X")


def test_empty_title_raises() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ToolbarSpecError, match="title"):
        ToolbarSpec(toolbar_id="tb.x", title="")


def test_visibility_mode_members() -> None:
    expected = {"ALWAYS", "COLLAPSIBLE", "AUTO_HIDE", "HIDDEN"}
    actual = {member.name for member in ToolbarVisibilityMode}
    assert actual == expected


def test_orientation_members() -> None:
    expected = {"HORIZONTAL", "VERTICAL"}
    actual = {member.name for member in ToolbarOrientation}
    assert actual == expected

"""Tests for ToolSpec."""

# mypy: ignore-errors
# This file intentionally mutates frozen dataclasses to test immutability.

from __future__ import annotations

import dataclasses

import pytest

from cranioz.ui.tools.specification import ToolSpec, ToolSpecError


def test_tool_spec_minimal() -> None:
    spec = ToolSpec(identifier="tool.a", label="A")
    assert spec.identifier == "tool.a"
    assert spec.label == "A"
    assert spec.description == ""
    assert spec.icon is None
    assert spec.category is None


def test_tool_spec_with_all_fields() -> None:
    spec = ToolSpec(
        identifier="tool.a",
        label="A",
        description="Does something",
        icon="tool_a",
        category="measurement",
    )
    assert spec.description == "Does something"
    assert spec.icon == "tool_a"
    assert spec.category == "measurement"


def test_empty_identifier_raises() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ToolSpecError, match="identifier"):
        ToolSpec(identifier="", label="A")


def test_empty_label_raises() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ToolSpecError, match="label"):
        ToolSpec(identifier="tool.a", label="")


def test_tool_spec_is_frozen() -> None:
    spec = ToolSpec(identifier="tool.a", label="A")
    with pytest.raises(dataclasses.FrozenInstanceError):
        spec.label = "B"

"""Tests for LogTool."""

from __future__ import annotations

import logging

from pytest import LogCaptureFixture

from cranioz.ui.tools.builtin.log_tool import LogTool
from cranioz.ui.tools.registry.tool_definition import ToolContext


def test_log_tool_metadata() -> None:
    tool = LogTool()
    assert tool.identifier == "tool.log"
    assert tool.label == "Log"
    assert tool.description == "Logs a message"
    assert tool.icon == "log"
    assert tool.category is None


def test_log_tool_is_available_default_true() -> None:
    tool = LogTool()
    assert tool.is_available(ToolContext()) is True


def test_log_tool_is_available_respects_context() -> None:
    tool = LogTool()
    ctx = ToolContext(extras={"available": False})
    assert tool.is_available(ctx) is False


def test_log_tool_is_enabled_default_true() -> None:
    tool = LogTool()
    assert tool.is_enabled(ToolContext()) is True


def test_log_tool_execute_logs_message(caplog: LogCaptureFixture) -> None:
    tool = LogTool(message="hello")
    with caplog.at_level(logging.INFO):
        tool.execute(ToolContext())
    assert "hello" in caplog.text

"""Tests for ToggleTool."""

from __future__ import annotations

from cranioz.ui.tools.builtin.toggle_tool import ToggleTool
from cranioz.ui.tools.registry.tool_definition import ToolContext


def test_toggle_tool_metadata() -> None:
    tool = ToggleTool()
    assert tool.identifier == "tool.toggle"
    assert tool.label == "Toggle"
    assert tool.icon == "toggle"


def test_toggle_default_state_is_false() -> None:
    tool = ToggleTool()
    assert tool.checked is False


def test_toggle_flips_state() -> None:
    tool = ToggleTool()
    assert tool.toggle() is True
    assert tool.checked is True
    assert tool.toggle() is False
    assert tool.checked is False


def test_toggle_initial_state_can_be_set() -> None:
    tool = ToggleTool(checked=True)
    assert tool.checked is True


def test_toggle_is_available_respects_context() -> None:
    tool = ToggleTool()
    assert tool.is_available(ToolContext(extras={"available": False})) is False


def test_toggle_is_enabled_respects_context() -> None:
    tool = ToggleTool()
    assert tool.is_enabled(ToolContext(extras={"enabled": False})) is False

FILE: tests/unit/ui/tools/registry/test_tool_definition.py
======================================================================

"""Tests for the Tool Protocol and ToolContext."""

from __future__ import annotations

from cranioz.ui.tools.registry.tool_definition import ToolContext


class ConcreteTool:
    """Minimal Tool implementation used only in tests."""

    identifier = "tool.log"
    label = "Log"
    description = "Logs a message"
    icon: str | None = None
    category: str | None = None

    def is_available(self, context: ToolContext) -> bool:
        return bool(context.extras.get("available", True))

    def is_enabled(self, context: ToolContext) -> bool:
        return bool(context.extras.get("enabled", True))


def test_tool_context_defaults() -> None:
    ctx = ToolContext()
    assert ctx.extras == {}


def test_tool_context_with_extras() -> None:
    ctx = ToolContext(extras={"enabled": False})
    assert ctx.extras["enabled"] is False


def test_concrete_tool_satisfies_protocol() -> None:
    tool = ConcreteTool()
    ctx = ToolContext()

    assert tool.identifier == "tool.log"
    assert tool.label == "Log"
    assert tool.description == "Logs a message"
    assert tool.icon is None
    assert tool.category is None
    assert tool.is_available(ctx) is True
    assert tool.is_enabled(ctx) is True


def test_tool_is_available() -> None:
    tool = ConcreteTool()
    assert tool.is_available(ToolContext()) is True


def test_tool_is_available_respects_context() -> None:
    tool = ConcreteTool()
    assert tool.is_available(ToolContext(extras={"available": False})) is False


def test_tool_is_enabled_defaults_to_true() -> None:
    tool = ConcreteTool()
    assert tool.is_enabled(ToolContext()) is True


def test_tool_is_enabled_respects_context() -> None:
    tool = ConcreteTool()
    assert tool.is_enabled(ToolContext(extras={"enabled": False})) is False


def test_object_without_methods_fails_protocol() -> None:
    class NotATool:
        identifier = "not.a.tool"

    not_a_tool = NotATool()
    assert not hasattr(not_a_tool, "is_available")
    assert not hasattr(not_a_tool, "is_enabled")



======================================================================
FILE: tests/unit/ui/tools/registry/test_tool_registry.py
======================================================================

"""Tests for ToolRegistry."""

from __future__ import annotations

import pytest

from cranioz.ui.tools.builtin.log_tool import LogTool
from cranioz.ui.tools.registry.tool_registry import (
    ToolRegistry,
    ToolRegistryError,
)


def _make_log() -> LogTool:
    return LogTool()


def test_register_and_resolve() -> None:
    registry = ToolRegistry()
    registry.register("tool.log", _make_log)
    factory = registry.resolve("tool.log")
    assert factory is _make_log


def test_register_idempotent_for_same_factory() -> None:
    registry = ToolRegistry()
    registry.register("tool.log", _make_log)
    registry.register("tool.log", _make_log)  # no raise
    assert registry.contains("tool.log")


def test_register_different_factory_for_same_id_raises() -> None:
    def other_factory() -> LogTool:
        return LogTool()

    registry = ToolRegistry()
    registry.register("tool.log", _make_log)
    with pytest.raises(ToolRegistryError, match="different factory"):
        registry.register("tool.log", other_factory)


def test_register_empty_id_raises() -> None:
    registry = ToolRegistry()
    with pytest.raises(ToolRegistryError, match="must not be empty"):
        registry.register("", _make_log)


def test_resolve_unknown_raises_keyerror() -> None:
    registry = ToolRegistry()
    with pytest.raises(KeyError, match="not registered"):
        registry.resolve("tool.unknown")


def test_create_returns_new_instance_each_time() -> None:
    registry = ToolRegistry()
    registry.register("tool.log", _make_log)
    a = registry.create("tool.log")
    b = registry.create("tool.log")
    assert a is not b
    assert isinstance(a, LogTool)
    assert isinstance(b, LogTool)


def test_unregister_removes() -> None:
    registry = ToolRegistry()
    registry.register("tool.log", _make_log)
    registry.unregister("tool.log")
    assert not registry.contains("tool.log")


def test_unregister_unknown_is_noop() -> None:
    registry = ToolRegistry()
    registry.unregister("tool.unknown")  # no raise


def test_identifiers_in_registration_order() -> None:
    registry = ToolRegistry()
    registry.register("tool.b", _make_log)
    registry.register("tool.a", _make_log)
    assert registry.identifiers() == ("tool.b", "tool.a")


def test_contains() -> None:
    registry = ToolRegistry()
    assert not registry.contains("tool.log")
    registry.register("tool.log", _make_log)
    assert registry.contains("tool.log")



======================================================================
FILE: tests/unit/ui/workspace/areas/test_area.py
======================================================================

"""Tests for Area."""

# mypy: ignore-errors
# This file exercises Qt widgets indirectly via DummyEditor.

from __future__ import annotations

import pytest

from cranioz.ui.editors.builtin.test_editor import DummyEditor
from cranioz.ui.workspace.areas.area import Area, AreaError
from cranioz.ui.workspace.areas.placement import Placement


def _area(
    area_id: str = "area.central",
    title: str = "Central",
    placement: Placement = Placement.CENTRAL,
) -> Area:
    return Area(area_id=area_id, title=title, placement=placement)


# ----- Construction ------------------------------------------------


def test_area_holds_id_and_title() -> None:
    area = _area(area_id="area.central", title="Central")
    assert area.area_id == "area.central"
    assert area.title == "Central"


def test_area_holds_placement() -> None:
    area = _area(placement=Placement.RIGHT)
    assert area.placement is Placement.RIGHT


def test_area_is_visible_by_default() -> None:
    area = _area()
    assert area.visible is True


def test_empty_area_id_raises() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(AreaError, match="area_id"):
        _area(area_id="")


def test_empty_title_raises() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(AreaError, match="title"):
        _area(title="")


# ----- Visibility ---------------------------------------------------


def test_hide_and_show() -> None:
    area = _area()
    area.hide()
    assert area.visible is False
    area.show()
    assert area.visible is True


# ----- Editor management -------------------------------------------


def test_new_area_has_no_editors() -> None:
    area = _area()
    assert area.editors() == ()
    assert area.editor_count() == 0
    assert area.active_editor_id is None
    assert area.is_empty() is True


def test_add_editor_appends() -> None:
    area = _area()
    editor = DummyEditor(editor_id="editor.a")
    area.add_editor(editor)
    assert area.editor_count() == 1
    assert area.get_editor("editor.a") is editor
    assert area.is_empty() is False


def test_first_editor_becomes_active() -> None:
    area = _area()
    editor = DummyEditor(editor_id="editor.a")
    area.add_editor(editor)
    assert area.active_editor_id == "editor.a"
    assert area.active_editor is editor


def test_add_editor_with_duplicate_id_raises() -> None:
    area = _area()
    area.add_editor(DummyEditor(editor_id="editor.a"))
    with pytest.raises(AreaError, match="already hosted"):
        area.add_editor(DummyEditor(editor_id="editor.a"))


def test_remove_editor_returns_it() -> None:
    area = _area()
    editor = DummyEditor(editor_id="editor.a")
    area.add_editor(editor)
    removed = area.remove_editor("editor.a")
    assert removed is editor
    assert area.editor_count() == 0
    assert area.is_empty() is True


def test_remove_unknown_editor_returns_none() -> None:
    area = _area()
    assert area.remove_editor("editor.unknown") is None


def test_removing_active_editor_promotes_next() -> None:
    area = _area()
    a = DummyEditor(editor_id="editor.a")
    b = DummyEditor(editor_id="editor.b")
    area.add_editor(a)
    area.add_editor(b)
    assert area.active_editor_id == "editor.a"

    area.remove_editor("editor.a")
    assert area.active_editor_id == "editor.b"


def test_removing_last_editor_clears_active_but_keeps_area() -> None:
    area = _area()
    area.add_editor(DummyEditor(editor_id="editor.a"))
    area.remove_editor("editor.a")
    assert area.active_editor_id is None
    assert area.is_empty() is True
    # The Area itself remains a valid container.
    assert area.area_id == "area.central"
    assert area.placement is Placement.CENTRAL


def test_removing_inactive_editor_keeps_active() -> None:
    area = _area()
    a = DummyEditor(editor_id="editor.a")
    b = DummyEditor(editor_id="editor.b")
    area.add_editor(a)
    area.add_editor(b)
    area.remove_editor("editor.b")
    assert area.active_editor_id == "editor.a"


# ----- Active Editor -------------------------------------------------


def test_set_active_editor() -> None:
    area = _area()
    a = DummyEditor(editor_id="editor.a")
    b = DummyEditor(editor_id="editor.b")
    area.add_editor(a)
    area.add_editor(b)
    area.set_active_editor("editor.b")
    assert area.active_editor_id == "editor.b"
    assert area.active_editor is b


def test_set_active_editor_unknown_raises() -> None:
    area = _area()
    with pytest.raises(AreaError, match="not hosted"):
        area.set_active_editor("editor.unknown")


# ----- Contains and count ------------------------------------------


def test_contains() -> None:
    area = _area()
    assert not area.contains("editor.a")
    area.add_editor(DummyEditor(editor_id="editor.a"))
    assert area.contains("editor.a")


# ----- Representation ----------------------------------------------


def test_repr_contains_key_fields() -> None:
    area = _area()
    area.add_editor(DummyEditor(editor_id="editor.a"))
    text = repr(area)
    assert "area.central" in text
    assert "central" in text
    assert "editor.a" in text



======================================================================
FILE: tests/unit/ui/workspace/areas/test_area_manager.py
======================================================================

"""Tests for AreaManager."""

# mypy: ignore-errors

from __future__ import annotations

import pytest

from cranioz.ui.workspace.areas.area import Area
from cranioz.ui.workspace.areas.area_manager import (
    AreaManager,
    AreaManagerError,
)
from cranioz.ui.workspace.areas.placement import Placement


def _area(
    area_id: str,
    placement: Placement = Placement.CENTRAL,
) -> Area:
    return Area(area_id=area_id, title=area_id, placement=placement)


# ----- Registration ------------------------------------------------


def test_register_and_get() -> None:
    manager = AreaManager()
    area = _area("area.central")
    manager.register(area)
    assert manager.get("area.central") is area


def test_register_same_instance_is_idempotent() -> None:
    manager = AreaManager()
    area = _area("area.central")
    manager.register(area)
    manager.register(area)  # no raise
    assert manager.area_count() == 1


def test_register_different_instance_same_id_raises() -> None:
    manager = AreaManager()
    a = _area("area.central")
    b = _area("area.central")
    manager.register(a)
    with pytest.raises(AreaManagerError, match="different Area"):
        manager.register(b)


# ----- Retrieval ---------------------------------------------------


def test_get_unknown_returns_none() -> None:
    manager = AreaManager()
    assert manager.get("area.unknown") is None


def test_require_returns_area() -> None:
    manager = AreaManager()
    area = _area("area.central")
    manager.register(area)
    assert manager.require("area.central") is area


def test_require_unknown_raises_keyerror() -> None:
    manager = AreaManager()
    with pytest.raises(KeyError, match="not registered"):
        manager.require("area.unknown")


def test_contains() -> None:
    manager = AreaManager()
    assert not manager.contains("area.central")
    manager.register(_area("area.central"))
    assert manager.contains("area.central")


# ----- Unregister --------------------------------------------------


def test_unregister_returns_area() -> None:
    manager = AreaManager()
    area = _area("area.central")
    manager.register(area)
    removed = manager.unregister("area.central")
    assert removed is area
    assert not manager.contains("area.central")


def test_unregister_unknown_returns_none() -> None:
    manager = AreaManager()
    assert manager.unregister("area.unknown") is None


# ----- Listing -----------------------------------------------------


def test_new_manager_is_empty() -> None:
    manager = AreaManager()
    assert manager.areas() == ()
    assert manager.area_count() == 0


def test_areas_preserve_insertion_order() -> None:
    manager = AreaManager()
    a = _area("area.left", Placement.LEFT)
    b = _area("area.central", Placement.CENTRAL)
    c = _area("area.right", Placement.RIGHT)
    manager.register(a)
    manager.register(b)
    manager.register(c)
    assert manager.areas() == (a, b, c)


def test_area_count() -> None:
    manager = AreaManager()
    manager.register(_area("area.a"))
    manager.register(_area("area.b"))
    assert manager.area_count() == 2


# ----- Placement-aware helpers -------------------------------------


def test_count_by_placement() -> None:
    manager = AreaManager()
    manager.register(_area("area.left", Placement.LEFT))
    manager.register(_area("area.central.a", Placement.CENTRAL))
    manager.register(_area("area.central.b", Placement.CENTRAL))
    manager.register(_area("area.right", Placement.RIGHT))

    assert manager.count_by_placement(Placement.LEFT) == 1
    assert manager.count_by_placement(Placement.CENTRAL) == 2
    assert manager.count_by_placement(Placement.RIGHT) == 1
    assert manager.count_by_placement(Placement.BOTTOM) == 0


def test_has_central() -> None:
    manager = AreaManager()
    assert manager.has_central() is False
    manager.register(_area("area.central"))
    assert manager.has_central() is True


# ----- Clear -------------------------------------------------------


def test_clear_removes_all() -> None:
    manager = AreaManager()
    manager.register(_area("area.a"))
    manager.register(_area("area.b"))
    manager.clear()
    assert manager.area_count() == 0
    assert manager.areas() == ()
    assert manager.has_central() is False


def test_clear_on_empty_is_noop() -> None:
    manager = AreaManager()
    manager.clear()
    assert manager.area_count() == 0


# ----- Representation ----------------------------------------------


def test_repr_contains_ids() -> None:
    manager = AreaManager()
    manager.register(_area("area.central"))
    assert "area.central" in repr(manager)

"""Tests for Placement."""

from __future__ import annotations

from cranioz.ui.workspace.areas.placement import Placement


def test_placement_has_expected_members() -> None:
    expected = {"LEFT", "CENTRAL", "RIGHT", "BOTTOM"}
    actual = {member.name for member in Placement}
    assert actual == expected


def test_placement_values_are_lowercase() -> None:
    for member in Placement:
        assert member.value == member.name.lower()


def test_placement_can_be_looked_up_by_value() -> None:
    assert Placement("central") is Placement.CENTRAL
    assert Placement("left") is Placement.LEFT
    assert Placement("right") is Placement.RIGHT
    assert Placement("bottom") is Placement.BOTTOM


def test_declaration_order_is_not_significant() -> None:
    """Guards against code depending on the enum's declaration order."""
    # Sort by value, not declaration order. This test will keep passing
    # even if the order of members in Placement changes.
    by_value = sorted(Placement, key=lambda p: p.value)
    assert by_value == [
        Placement.BOTTOM,
        Placement.CENTRAL,
        Placement.LEFT,
        Placement.RIGHT,
    ]



======================================================================
FILE: tests/unit/ui/workspace/layout/test_layout_tree.py
======================================================================

"""Tests for LayoutTree."""

# mypy: ignore-errors
# This file exercises the structural layout tree with concrete Areas.

from __future__ import annotations

import pytest

from cranioz.ui.workspace.areas.area import Area
from cranioz.ui.workspace.areas.placement import Placement
from cranioz.ui.workspace.layout.layout_tree import (
    LayoutTree,
    LayoutTreeError,
    Leaf,
    Split,
)
from cranioz.ui.workspace.specs.area_spec import AreaSpec
from cranioz.ui.workspace.specs.editor_layout_spec import (
    EditorLayoutSpec,
    SplitDirection,
)

# ----- Helpers -----------------------------------------------------


def _area(
    area_id: str,
    placement: Placement = Placement.CENTRAL,
) -> Area:
    return Area(area_id=area_id, title=area_id, placement=placement)


def _spec(area_id: str, placement: Placement, editor_id: str) -> AreaSpec:
    return AreaSpec(
        area_id=area_id,
        title=area_id,
        placement=placement,
        editor_layout=EditorLayoutSpec.leaf(editor_id),
    )


def _areas_from_specs(specs: tuple[AreaSpec, ...]) -> dict[str, Area]:
    return {s.area_id: _area(s.area_id, s.placement) for s in specs}


# ----- Nodes -------------------------------------------------------


def test_leaf_holds_area() -> None:
    a = _area("area.central")
    leaf = Leaf(area=a)
    assert leaf.area is a


def test_split_requires_two_children() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(LayoutTreeError, match="at least two children"):
        Split(
            direction=SplitDirection.HORIZONTAL,
            children=(Leaf(area=_area("area.a")),),
        )


# ----- from_specs — single central --------------------------------


def test_from_specs_single_central_is_leaf() -> None:
    spec = _spec("area.central", Placement.CENTRAL, "editor.a")
    areas = _areas_from_specs((spec,))
    tree = LayoutTree.from_specs((spec,), areas)
    assert isinstance(tree.root, Leaf)
    assert tree.root.area.area_id == "area.central"


# ----- from_specs — left + central + right ------------------------


def test_from_specs_left_central_right_is_horizontal_split() -> None:
    specs = (
        _spec("area.left", Placement.LEFT, "editor.left"),
        _spec("area.central", Placement.CENTRAL, "editor.central"),
        _spec("area.right", Placement.RIGHT, "editor.right"),
    )
    areas = _areas_from_specs(specs)
    tree = LayoutTree.from_specs(specs, areas)

    assert isinstance(tree.root, Split)
    assert tree.root.direction is SplitDirection.HORIZONTAL
    assert len(tree.root.children) == 3
    assert [leaf.area.area_id for leaf in tree.leaves()] == [
        "area.left",
        "area.central",
        "area.right",
    ]


def test_from_specs_central_only_with_two_is_horizontal() -> None:
    specs = (
        _spec("area.central.a", Placement.CENTRAL, "editor.a"),
        _spec("area.central.b", Placement.CENTRAL, "editor.b"),
    )
    areas = _areas_from_specs(specs)
    tree = LayoutTree.from_specs(specs, areas)

    assert isinstance(tree.root, Split)
    assert tree.root.direction is SplitDirection.HORIZONTAL


# ----- from_specs — with bottom ------------------------------------


def test_from_specs_with_bottom_is_vertical_split() -> None:
    specs = (
        _spec("area.central", Placement.CENTRAL, "editor.central"),
        _spec("area.bottom", Placement.BOTTOM, "editor.bottom"),
    )
    areas = _areas_from_specs(specs)
    tree = LayoutTree.from_specs(specs, areas)

    assert isinstance(tree.root, Split)
    assert tree.root.direction is SplitDirection.VERTICAL
    assert len(tree.root.children) == 2


def test_from_specs_four_areas_has_nested_split() -> None:
    specs = (
        _spec("area.left", Placement.LEFT, "editor.left"),
        _spec("area.central", Placement.CENTRAL, "editor.central"),
        _spec("area.right", Placement.RIGHT, "editor.right"),
        _spec("area.bottom", Placement.BOTTOM, "editor.bottom"),
    )
    areas = _areas_from_specs(specs)
    tree = LayoutTree.from_specs(specs, areas)

    assert isinstance(tree.root, Split)
    assert tree.root.direction is SplitDirection.VERTICAL
    top, bottom = tree.root.children
    assert isinstance(top, Split)
    assert top.direction is SplitDirection.HORIZONTAL
    assert isinstance(bottom, Leaf)


# ----- from_specs — errors ----------------------------------------


def test_from_specs_without_central_raises() -> None:
    specs = (
        _spec("area.left", Placement.LEFT, "editor.left"),
        _spec("area.right", Placement.RIGHT, "editor.right"),
    )
    areas = _areas_from_specs(specs)
    with pytest.raises(LayoutTreeError, match="CENTRAL"):
        LayoutTree.from_specs(specs, areas)


def test_from_specs_empty_raises() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(LayoutTreeError, match="at least one spec"):
        LayoutTree.from_specs((), {})


def test_from_specs_missing_area_raises() -> None:
    specs = (_spec("area.central", Placement.CENTRAL, "editor.central"),)
    with pytest.raises(LayoutTreeError, match="unknown area_id"):
        LayoutTree.from_specs(specs, {})


# ----- Traversal ---------------------------------------------------


def test_areas_returns_in_depth_first_order() -> None:
    specs = (
        _spec("area.left", Placement.LEFT, "editor.left"),
        _spec("area.central", Placement.CENTRAL, "editor.central"),
        _spec("area.right", Placement.RIGHT, "editor.right"),
    )
    areas = _areas_from_specs(specs)
    tree = LayoutTree.from_specs(specs, areas)

    assert [a.area_id for a in tree.areas()] == [
        "area.left",
        "area.central",
        "area.right",
    ]


def test_find_returns_leaf() -> None:
    specs = (
        _spec("area.left", Placement.LEFT, "editor.left"),
        _spec("area.central", Placement.CENTRAL, "editor.central"),
    )
    areas = _areas_from_specs(specs)
    tree = LayoutTree.from_specs(specs, areas)

    leaf = tree.find("area.central")
    assert leaf is not None
    assert leaf.area.area_id == "area.central"


def test_find_unknown_returns_none() -> None:
    specs = (_spec("area.central", Placement.CENTRAL, "editor.central"),)
    areas = _areas_from_specs(specs)
    tree = LayoutTree.from_specs(specs, areas)

    assert tree.find("area.unknown") is None


def test_contains() -> None:
    specs = (_spec("area.central", Placement.CENTRAL, "editor.central"),)
    areas = _areas_from_specs(specs)
    tree = LayoutTree.from_specs(specs, areas)

    assert tree.contains("area.central")
    assert not tree.contains("area.other")


def test_area_count() -> None:
    specs = (
        _spec("area.left", Placement.LEFT, "editor.left"),
        _spec("area.central", Placement.CENTRAL, "editor.central"),
        _spec("area.right", Placement.RIGHT, "editor.right"),
    )
    areas = _areas_from_specs(specs)
    tree = LayoutTree.from_specs(specs, areas)

    assert tree.area_count() == 3


# ----- Placement propagation ---------------------------------------


def test_leaves_carry_areas_with_correct_placement() -> None:
    specs = (
        _spec("area.left", Placement.LEFT, "editor.left"),
        _spec("area.central", Placement.CENTRAL, "editor.central"),
        _spec("area.right", Placement.RIGHT, "editor.right"),
        _spec("area.bottom", Placement.BOTTOM, "editor.bottom"),
    )
    areas = _areas_from_specs(specs)
    tree = LayoutTree.from_specs(specs, areas)

    by_id = {leaf.area.area_id: leaf.area.placement for leaf in tree.leaves()}
    assert by_id["area.left"] is Placement.LEFT
    assert by_id["area.central"] is Placement.CENTRAL
    assert by_id["area.right"] is Placement.RIGHT
    assert by_id["area.bottom"] is Placement.BOTTOM


# ----- find_regions -------------------------------------------------


def test_find_regions_single_central() -> None:
    specs = (_spec("area.central", Placement.CENTRAL, "editor.central"),)
    areas = _areas_from_specs(specs)
    tree = LayoutTree.from_specs(specs, areas)

    regions = tree.find_regions()

    assert len(regions) == 1
    assert regions[0].region_id == "central"
    assert regions[0].semantic_role is Placement.CENTRAL
    assert [a.area_id for a in regions[0].areas] == ["area.central"]


def test_find_regions_left_central_right() -> None:
    specs = (
        _spec("area.left", Placement.LEFT, "editor.left"),
        _spec("area.central", Placement.CENTRAL, "editor.central"),
        _spec("area.right", Placement.RIGHT, "editor.right"),
    )
    areas = _areas_from_specs(specs)
    tree = LayoutTree.from_specs(specs, areas)

    regions = tree.find_regions()

    assert [r.region_id for r in regions] == ["left", "central", "right"]
    assert [r.semantic_role for r in regions] == [
        Placement.LEFT,
        Placement.CENTRAL,
        Placement.RIGHT,
    ]


def test_find_regions_returns_in_stable_order() -> None:
    """The order is LEFT, CENTRAL, RIGHT, BOTTOM regardless of spec order."""
    specs = (
        _spec("area.bottom", Placement.BOTTOM, "editor.bottom"),
        _spec("area.right", Placement.RIGHT, "editor.right"),
        _spec("area.central", Placement.CENTRAL, "editor.central"),
        _spec("area.left", Placement.LEFT, "editor.left"),
    )
    areas = _areas_from_specs(specs)
    tree = LayoutTree.from_specs(specs, areas)

    regions = tree.find_regions()

    assert [r.region_id for r in regions] == [
        "left",
        "central",
        "right",
        "bottom",
    ]


def test_find_regions_groups_multiple_central_areas() -> None:
    """A complex central region may contain more than one Area."""
    specs = (
        _spec("area.central.a", Placement.CENTRAL, "editor.a"),
        _spec("area.central.b", Placement.CENTRAL, "editor.b"),
    )
    areas = _areas_from_specs(specs)
    tree = LayoutTree.from_specs(specs, areas)

    regions = tree.find_regions()

    assert len(regions) == 1
    assert regions[0].region_id == "central"
    assert [a.area_id for a in regions[0].areas] == [
        "area.central.a",
        "area.central.b",
    ]


def test_find_regions_skips_placements_with_no_areas() -> None:
    """Only placements that have at least one Area produce a Region."""
    specs = (
        _spec("area.central", Placement.CENTRAL, "editor.central"),
        _spec("area.bottom", Placement.BOTTOM, "editor.bottom"),
    )
    areas = _areas_from_specs(specs)
    tree = LayoutTree.from_specs(specs, areas)

    regions = tree.find_regions()

    assert [r.region_id for r in regions] == ["central", "bottom"]
    # "left" and "right" do not appear because there is no Area there.


def test_find_regions_bounds_are_placeholders() -> None:
    """find_regions() does not compute geometry.

    Every returned Region has a placeholder Bounds (zero rectangle).
    The LayoutRenderer will fill the real Bounds once widgets exist.
    """
    specs = (
        _spec("area.left", Placement.LEFT, "editor.left"),
        _spec("area.central", Placement.CENTRAL, "editor.central"),
    )
    areas = _areas_from_specs(specs)
    tree = LayoutTree.from_specs(specs, areas)

    regions = tree.find_regions()

    for region in regions:
        assert region.bounds.is_placeholder() is True


def test_find_regions_every_area_belongs_to_exactly_one_region() -> None:
    specs = (
        _spec("area.left", Placement.LEFT, "editor.left"),
        _spec("area.central", Placement.CENTRAL, "editor.central"),
        _spec("area.right", Placement.RIGHT, "editor.right"),
        _spec("area.bottom", Placement.BOTTOM, "editor.bottom"),
    )
    areas = _areas_from_specs(specs)
    tree = LayoutTree.from_specs(specs, areas)

    regions = tree.find_regions()

    seen: list[str] = []
    for region in regions:
        seen.extend(a.area_id for a in region.areas)
    assert sorted(seen) == sorted(["area.left", "area.central", "area.right", "area.bottom"])
    assert len(seen) == len(set(seen))  # no duplicates


def test_find_regions_areas_are_in_depth_first_order() -> None:
    specs = (
        _spec("area.left.a", Placement.LEFT, "editor.a"),
        _spec("area.left.b", Placement.LEFT, "editor.b"),
        _spec("area.central", Placement.CENTRAL, "editor.central"),
    )
    areas = _areas_from_specs(specs)
    tree = LayoutTree.from_specs(specs, areas)

    regions = tree.find_regions()

    left = next(r for r in regions if r.region_id == "left")
    assert [a.area_id for a in left.areas] == [
        "area.left.a",
        "area.left.b",
    ]



======================================================================
FILE: tests/unit/ui/workspace/overlays/test_overlay.py
======================================================================

"""Tests for the Overlay ABC.

The Overlay ABC is an abstract interface. To exercise it we define
a concrete subclass, ``FakeOverlay``, that implements every abstract
method with the minimal behaviour required by the contract.

The FakeOverlay uses a real QWidget (a QLabel). The tests that
instantiate it use ``qtbot`` and therefore require a QApplication.
The tests that only inspect the abstract methods do not create a
widget and could in principle run without Qt; we keep them together
for clarity.

These tests verify:

- The ABC cannot be instantiated directly.
- A concrete subclass must implement every abstract method.
- overlay_id and target_region are readable.
- widget() returns the QWidget provided by the subclass.
- reposition() records the Bounds it receives.
- set_visible() / is_visible() work as expected.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import pytest
from PySide6.QtWidgets import QLabel, QWidget
from pytestqt.qtbot import QtBot

from cranioz.ui.workspace.areas.placement import Placement
from cranioz.ui.workspace.overlays.overlay import Overlay
from cranioz.ui.workspace.overlays.region import Bounds

# ----- Concrete test implementation ---------------------------------


class FakeOverlay(Overlay):
    """Minimal concrete Overlay used only in tests."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        overlay_id: str = "overlay.test",
        target_region: Placement = Placement.CENTRAL,
        widget: QWidget | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        self._overlay_id = overlay_id
        self._target_region = target_region
        self._widget = widget if widget is not None else QLabel("Fake")
        self._visible = True
        self._last_bounds: Bounds | None = None

    @property
    def overlay_id(self) -> str:
        return self._overlay_id

    @property
    def target_region(self) -> Placement:
        return self._target_region

    def widget(self) -> QWidget:
        return self._widget

    def reposition(self, bounds: Bounds) -> None:
        self._last_bounds = bounds

    def set_visible(self, visible: bool) -> None:
        self._visible = visible
        self._widget.setVisible(visible)

    def is_visible(self) -> bool:
        return self._visible

    # ----- Test-only accessors ---------------------------------------

    @property
    def last_bounds(self) -> Bounds | None:
        return self._last_bounds


# ----- Abstractness -------------------------------------------------


def test_overlay_is_abstract() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(TypeError):
        Overlay()  # type: ignore[abstract]


def test_concrete_overlay_can_be_instantiated(qtbot: QtBot) -> None:
    overlay = FakeOverlay()
    qtbot.addWidget(overlay.widget())
    assert isinstance(overlay, Overlay)


# ----- Identity -----------------------------------------------------


def test_overlay_id_is_readable(qtbot: QtBot) -> None:
    overlay = FakeOverlay(overlay_id="overlay.top_bar")
    qtbot.addWidget(overlay.widget())
    assert overlay.overlay_id == "overlay.top_bar"


def test_target_region_is_readable(qtbot: QtBot) -> None:
    overlay = FakeOverlay(target_region=Placement.CENTRAL)
    qtbot.addWidget(overlay.widget())
    assert overlay.target_region is Placement.CENTRAL


def test_target_region_accepts_all_placements(qtbot: QtBot) -> None:
    for placement in Placement:
        overlay = FakeOverlay(target_region=placement)
        qtbot.addWidget(overlay.widget())
        assert overlay.target_region is placement


# ----- Widget -------------------------------------------------------


def test_widget_returns_qwidget(qtbot: QtBot) -> None:
    overlay = FakeOverlay()
    widget = overlay.widget()
    qtbot.addWidget(widget)
    assert isinstance(widget, QWidget)


def test_widget_is_stable_across_calls(qtbot: QtBot) -> None:
    overlay = FakeOverlay()
    first = overlay.widget()
    second = overlay.widget()
    qtbot.addWidget(first)
    assert first is second


# ----- Reposition ---------------------------------------------------


def test_reposition_records_bounds(qtbot: QtBot) -> None:
    overlay = FakeOverlay()
    qtbot.addWidget(overlay.widget())

    bounds = Bounds(x=100.0, y=50.0, width=800.0, height=600.0)
    overlay.reposition(bounds)

    assert overlay.last_bounds == bounds


def test_reposition_accepts_placeholder_bounds(qtbot: QtBot) -> None:
    overlay = FakeOverlay()
    qtbot.addWidget(overlay.widget())

    overlay.reposition(Bounds())
    assert overlay.last_bounds is not None
    assert overlay.last_bounds.is_placeholder() is True


def test_reposition_can_be_called_multiple_times(qtbot: QtBot) -> None:
    overlay = FakeOverlay()
    qtbot.addWidget(overlay.widget())

    first = Bounds(x=0.0, y=0.0, width=100.0, height=100.0)
    second = Bounds(x=10.0, y=10.0, width=200.0, height=200.0)

    overlay.reposition(first)
    overlay.reposition(second)

    assert overlay.last_bounds == second


# ----- Visibility ---------------------------------------------------


def test_overlay_starts_visible(qtbot: QtBot) -> None:
    overlay = FakeOverlay()
    qtbot.addWidget(overlay.widget())
    assert overlay.is_visible() is True


def test_set_visible_false_hides_overlay(qtbot: QtBot) -> None:
    overlay = FakeOverlay()
    qtbot.addWidget(overlay.widget())

    overlay.set_visible(False)

    assert overlay.is_visible() is False
    assert overlay.widget().isVisible() is False


def test_set_visible_true_shows_overlay(qtbot: QtBot) -> None:
    overlay = FakeOverlay()
    qtbot.addWidget(overlay.widget())

    overlay.set_visible(False)
    overlay.set_visible(True)

    assert overlay.is_visible() is True


def test_visibility_toggle_does_not_destroy_widget(qtbot: QtBot) -> None:
    overlay = FakeOverlay()
    widget = overlay.widget()
    qtbot.addWidget(widget)

    overlay.set_visible(False)
    overlay.set_visible(True)

    # The widget instance is the same throughout.
    assert overlay.widget() is widget



======================================================================
FILE: tests/unit/ui/workspace/overlays/test_overlay_manager.py
======================================================================

"""Tests for OverlayManager.

The OverlayManager coordinates a set of Overlays: registration,
lookup, and repositioning when the layout changes. It does not
create Overlays and does not compute geometry.

These tests use a FakeOverlay: a concrete Overlay subclass that
records the Bounds it receives, so we can assert that
``reposition_all()`` routed the correct Bounds to the correct
Overlay.

The FakeOverlay uses a real QWidget (QLabel) because the Overlay
contract requires ``widget()`` to return a QWidget. The tests use
``qtbot`` and therefore a QApplication.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import pytest
from PySide6.QtWidgets import QLabel, QWidget
from pytestqt.qtbot import QtBot

from cranioz.ui.workspace.areas.area import Area
from cranioz.ui.workspace.areas.placement import Placement
from cranioz.ui.workspace.overlays.overlay import Overlay
from cranioz.ui.workspace.overlays.overlay_manager import (
    OverlayManager,
    OverlayManagerError,
)
from cranioz.ui.workspace.overlays.region import Bounds, Region

# ----- Test helpers -------------------------------------------------


class FakeOverlay(Overlay):
    """Minimal Overlay that records the last Bounds it received."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        overlay_id: str,
        target_region: Placement = Placement.CENTRAL,
        widget: QWidget | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        self._overlay_id = overlay_id
        self._target_region = target_region
        self._widget = widget if widget is not None else QLabel(overlay_id)
        self._visible = True
        self._last_bounds: Bounds | None = None

    @property
    def overlay_id(self) -> str:
        return self._overlay_id

    @property
    def target_region(self) -> Placement:
        return self._target_region

    def widget(self) -> QWidget:
        return self._widget

    def reposition(self, bounds: Bounds) -> None:
        self._last_bounds = bounds

    def set_visible(self, visible: bool) -> None:
        self._visible = visible
        self._widget.setVisible(visible)

    def is_visible(self) -> bool:
        return self._visible

    @property
    def last_bounds(self) -> Bounds | None:
        return self._last_bounds


def _region(
    region_id: str,
    placement: Placement,
    bounds: Bounds | None = None,
) -> Region:
    """Build a Region with a single synthetic Area placeholder.

    The OverlayManager never inspects the Areas, only the
    ``semantic_role`` and ``bounds``. A synthetic Area is enough.
    """

    area = Area(area_id=f"area.{region_id}", title=region_id, placement=placement)
    return Region(
        region_id=region_id,
        bounds=bounds if bounds is not None else Bounds(),
        areas=(area,),
        semantic_role=placement,
    )


@pytest.fixture
def manager() -> OverlayManager:
    return OverlayManager()


@pytest.fixture
def make_overlay(qtbot: QtBot):
    """Factory to build FakeOverlay instances and register their widgets."""

    def _factory(
        overlay_id: str,
        target_region: Placement = Placement.CENTRAL,
    ) -> FakeOverlay:
        overlay = FakeOverlay(
            overlay_id=overlay_id,
            target_region=target_region,
        )
        qtbot.addWidget(overlay.widget())
        return overlay

    return _factory


# ----- Registration -------------------------------------------------


def test_new_manager_is_empty(manager: OverlayManager) -> None:
    assert manager.overlays() == ()
    assert manager.overlay_count() == 0


def test_register_adds_overlay(manager: OverlayManager, make_overlay) -> None:
    overlay = make_overlay("overlay.top_bar")
    manager.register(overlay)

    assert manager.contains("overlay.top_bar")
    assert manager.overlay_count() == 1
    assert manager.get("overlay.top_bar") is overlay


def test_register_same_instance_is_idempotent(manager: OverlayManager, make_overlay) -> None:
    overlay = make_overlay("overlay.top_bar")
    manager.register(overlay)
    manager.register(overlay)
    assert manager.overlay_count() == 1


def test_register_different_instance_same_id_raises(manager: OverlayManager, make_overlay) -> None:
    a = make_overlay("overlay.top_bar")
    b = make_overlay("overlay.top_bar")
    manager.register(a)
    with pytest.raises(OverlayManagerError, match="different Overlay"):
        manager.register(b)


def test_register_empty_id_raises(manager: OverlayManager, qtbot: QtBot) -> None:
    # FakeOverlay permits empty ids for test purposes; the manager
    # must reject them.
    overlay = FakeOverlay(overlay_id="")
    qtbot.addWidget(overlay.widget())

    with pytest.raises(OverlayManagerError, match="must not be empty"):
        manager.register(overlay)


def test_registration_order_is_preserved(manager: OverlayManager, make_overlay) -> None:
    a = make_overlay("overlay.a")
    b = make_overlay("overlay.b")
    c = make_overlay("overlay.c")
    manager.register(a)
    manager.register(b)
    manager.register(c)

    assert manager.overlays() == (a, b, c)


# ----- Lookup -------------------------------------------------------


def test_get_unknown_returns_none(manager: OverlayManager) -> None:
    assert manager.get("overlay.unknown") is None


def test_require_returns_overlay(manager: OverlayManager, make_overlay) -> None:
    overlay = make_overlay("overlay.top_bar")
    manager.register(overlay)
    assert manager.require("overlay.top_bar") is overlay


def test_require_unknown_raises(manager: OverlayManager) -> None:
    with pytest.raises(KeyError, match="not registered"):
        manager.require("overlay.unknown")


def test_contains(manager: OverlayManager, make_overlay) -> None:
    assert manager.contains("overlay.x") is False
    manager.register(make_overlay("overlay.x"))
    assert manager.contains("overlay.x") is True


# ----- Unregister ---------------------------------------------------


def test_unregister_returns_overlay(manager: OverlayManager, make_overlay) -> None:
    overlay = make_overlay("overlay.a")
    manager.register(overlay)

    removed = manager.unregister("overlay.a")
    assert removed is overlay
    assert not manager.contains("overlay.a")


def test_unregister_unknown_returns_none(manager: OverlayManager) -> None:
    assert manager.unregister("overlay.unknown") is None


def test_unregister_does_not_destroy_widget(
    manager: OverlayManager, make_overlay, qtbot: QtBot
) -> None:
    overlay = make_overlay("overlay.a")
    widget = overlay.widget()
    qtbot.addWidget(widget)

    manager.register(overlay)
    manager.unregister("overlay.a")

    # The widget instance is unchanged; the manager only dropped
    # its reference to the Overlay.
    assert overlay.widget() is widget


# ----- Clear --------------------------------------------------------


def test_clear_removes_all(manager: OverlayManager, make_overlay) -> None:
    manager.register(make_overlay("overlay.a"))
    manager.register(make_overlay("overlay.b"))

    manager.clear()

    assert manager.overlay_count() == 0
    assert manager.overlays() == ()


def test_clear_on_empty_is_noop(manager: OverlayManager) -> None:
    manager.clear()
    assert manager.overlay_count() == 0


# ----- Repositioning ------------------------------------------------


def test_reposition_all_with_no_overlays_is_noop(
    manager: OverlayManager,
) -> None:
    manager.reposition_all(())  # must not raise


def test_reposition_all_with_no_regions_is_noop(manager: OverlayManager, make_overlay) -> None:
    overlay = make_overlay("overlay.a", target_region=Placement.CENTRAL)
    manager.register(overlay)

    manager.reposition_all(())

    assert overlay.last_bounds is None


def test_reposition_all_routes_bounds_by_placement(manager: OverlayManager, make_overlay) -> None:
    central = make_overlay("overlay.central", target_region=Placement.CENTRAL)
    bottom = make_overlay("overlay.bottom", target_region=Placement.BOTTOM)
    manager.register(central)
    manager.register(bottom)

    central_bounds = Bounds(x=100.0, y=50.0, width=800.0, height=600.0)
    bottom_bounds = Bounds(x=0.0, y=650.0, width=1000.0, height=200.0)
    regions = (
        _region("central", Placement.CENTRAL, central_bounds),
        _region("bottom", Placement.BOTTOM, bottom_bounds),
    )

    manager.reposition_all(regions)

    assert central.last_bounds == central_bounds
    assert bottom.last_bounds == bottom_bounds


def test_reposition_all_leaves_overlays_without_matching_region(
    manager: OverlayManager, make_overlay
) -> None:
    # The overlay targets CENTRAL, but the current layout has no
    # CENTRAL region.
    overlay = make_overlay("overlay.central", target_region=Placement.CENTRAL)
    manager.register(overlay)

    regions = (_region("left", Placement.LEFT),)
    manager.reposition_all(regions)

    assert overlay.last_bounds is None


def test_reposition_all_skips_regions_without_semantic_role(
    manager: OverlayManager, make_overlay
) -> None:
    overlay = make_overlay("overlay.central", target_region=Placement.CENTRAL)
    manager.register(overlay)

    # A Region with semantic_role=None should never be used to route
    # bounds; find_regions() always sets it, but defensive code is
    # still correct.
    area = Area(area_id="area.x", title="x", placement=Placement.CENTRAL)
    orphan = Region(
        region_id="orphan",
        bounds=Bounds(x=1.0, y=1.0, width=1.0, height=1.0),
        areas=(area,),
        semantic_role=None,
    )
    manager.reposition_all((orphan,))

    assert overlay.last_bounds is None


def test_reposition_all_can_be_called_multiple_times(manager: OverlayManager, make_overlay) -> None:
    overlay = make_overlay("overlay.central", target_region=Placement.CENTRAL)
    manager.register(overlay)

    first = Bounds(x=0.0, y=0.0, width=100.0, height=100.0)
    second = Bounds(x=10.0, y=10.0, width=200.0, height=200.0)

    manager.reposition_all((_region("central", Placement.CENTRAL, first),))
    assert overlay.last_bounds == first

    manager.reposition_all((_region("central", Placement.CENTRAL, second),))
    assert overlay.last_bounds == second


def test_reposition_all_uses_first_matching_region_if_duplicated(
    manager: OverlayManager, make_overlay
) -> None:
    # A pathological case: two regions with the same semantic_role.
    # The manager should deterministically pick one (the first seen).
    overlay = make_overlay("overlay.central", target_region=Placement.CENTRAL)
    manager.register(overlay)

    first = Bounds(x=0.0, y=0.0, width=10.0, height=10.0)
    second = Bounds(x=0.0, y=0.0, width=20.0, height=20.0)

    manager.reposition_all(
        (
            _region("central.a", Placement.CENTRAL, first),
            _region("central.b", Placement.CENTRAL, second),
        )
    )

    assert overlay.last_bounds == first


# ----- Representation -----------------------------------------------


def test_repr_contains_registered_ids(manager: OverlayManager, make_overlay) -> None:
    manager.register(make_overlay("overlay.a"))
    text = repr(manager)
    assert "overlay.a" in text



======================================================================
FILE: tests/unit/ui/workspace/overlays/test_region.py
======================================================================

"""Tests for Region and Bounds.

Region and Bounds are pure domain structures. They do not depend on
Qt, and these tests run without a QApplication.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import dataclasses

import pytest

from cranioz.ui.workspace.areas.area import Area
from cranioz.ui.workspace.areas.placement import Placement
from cranioz.ui.workspace.overlays.region import Bounds, Region

# ----- Helpers ------------------------------------------------------


def _area(
    area_id: str,
    placement: Placement = Placement.CENTRAL,
) -> Area:
    return Area(area_id=area_id, title=area_id, placement=placement)


# ----- Bounds -------------------------------------------------------


def test_bounds_defaults_to_zero_rectangle() -> None:
    b = Bounds()
    assert b.x == 0.0
    assert b.y == 0.0
    assert b.width == 0.0
    assert b.height == 0.0


def test_bounds_holds_all_fields() -> None:
    b = Bounds(x=10.0, y=20.0, width=100.0, height=50.0)
    assert b.x == 10.0
    assert b.y == 20.0
    assert b.width == 100.0
    assert b.height == 50.0


def test_bounds_is_frozen() -> None:
    b = Bounds()
    with pytest.raises(dataclasses.FrozenInstanceError):
        b.x = 1.0  # type: ignore[misc]


def test_bounds_default_is_placeholder() -> None:
    assert Bounds().is_placeholder() is True


def test_bounds_with_nonzero_values_is_not_placeholder() -> None:
    assert Bounds(x=1.0).is_placeholder() is False
    assert Bounds(width=1.0).is_placeholder() is False


def test_bounds_negative_width_raises() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="width"):
        Bounds(width=-1.0)


def test_bounds_negative_height_raises() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="height"):
        Bounds(height=-1.0)


def test_bounds_zero_width_is_valid() -> None:
    # A zero-width Bounds is allowed (e.g. a degenerate rectangle).
    Bounds(width=0.0)


def test_bounds_equality() -> None:
    a = Bounds(1.0, 2.0, 3.0, 4.0)
    b = Bounds(1.0, 2.0, 3.0, 4.0)
    assert a == b


# ----- Region -------------------------------------------------------


def test_region_holds_all_fields() -> None:
    areas = (_area("area.left", Placement.LEFT),)
    region = Region(
        region_id="left",
        bounds=Bounds(),
        areas=areas,
        semantic_role=Placement.LEFT,
    )
    assert region.region_id == "left"
    assert region.areas == areas
    assert region.semantic_role is Placement.LEFT


def test_region_empty_id_raises() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="region_id"):
        Region(
            region_id="",
            bounds=Bounds(),
            areas=(_area("area.a"),),
            semantic_role=Placement.CENTRAL,
        )


def test_region_with_no_areas_raises() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="at least one Area"):
        Region(
            region_id="central",
            bounds=Bounds(),
            areas=(),
            semantic_role=Placement.CENTRAL,
        )


def test_region_is_frozen() -> None:
    region = Region(
        region_id="central",
        bounds=Bounds(),
        areas=(_area("area.central"),),
        semantic_role=Placement.CENTRAL,
    )
    with pytest.raises(dataclasses.FrozenInstanceError):
        region.region_id = "other"  # type: ignore[misc]


def test_region_area_count() -> None:
    region = Region(
        region_id="central",
        bounds=Bounds(),
        areas=(
            _area("area.central.a"),
            _area("area.central.b"),
        ),
        semantic_role=Placement.CENTRAL,
    )
    assert region.area_count() == 2


def test_region_contains() -> None:
    region = Region(
        region_id="central",
        bounds=Bounds(),
        areas=(_area("area.central"),),
        semantic_role=Placement.CENTRAL,
    )
    assert region.contains("area.central") is True
    assert region.contains("area.other") is False


def test_region_with_bounds_returns_new_instance() -> None:
    region = Region(
        region_id="central",
        bounds=Bounds(),
        areas=(_area("area.central"),),
        semantic_role=Placement.CENTRAL,
    )
    real_bounds = Bounds(x=100.0, y=0.0, width=800.0, height=600.0)
    updated = region.with_bounds(real_bounds)

    assert updated is not region
    assert updated.bounds == real_bounds
    assert region.bounds.is_placeholder() is True  # original unchanged
    assert updated.areas == region.areas
    assert updated.semantic_role is region.semantic_role


def test_region_semantic_role_can_be_none() -> None:
    # Allowed for future flexibility; today always set by find_regions.
    region = Region(
        region_id="custom",
        bounds=Bounds(),
        areas=(_area("area.custom"),),
        semantic_role=None,
    )
    assert region.semantic_role is None


def test_region_repr_mentions_key_fields() -> None:
    region = Region(
        region_id="central",
        bounds=Bounds(),
        areas=(_area("area.central"),),
        semantic_role=Placement.CENTRAL,
    )
    text = repr(region)
    assert "central" in text
    assert "area.central" in text
    assert "placeholder" in text



======================================================================
FILE: tests/unit/ui/workspace/overlays/test_top_overlay.py
======================================================================

"""Tests for TopOverlay.

The TopOverlay is the first concrete Overlay. It materializes the
Top Bar geometry: a fixed-height widget anchored to the top of the
Central Region with small lateral and top margins.

The tests verify:

- Identity: overlay_id and target_region are stable constants.
- Widget: widget() returns the same QWidget across calls.
- Fixed height: the widget has a fixed height equal to the
  configured one.
- Reposition: given Bounds, the widget's geometry is computed with
  the documented margins and height.
- Narrow regions: the width is clamped to 0 instead of going
  negative.
- Visibility: set_visible toggles the widget's visibility without
  destroying it.
- Custom parameters: non-default height and margins work.
- content_layout(): exposes a QLayout for future content.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import pytest
from PySide6.QtWidgets import QFrame, QLayout, QWidget
from pytestqt.qtbot import QtBot

from cranioz.ui.workspace.areas.placement import Placement
from cranioz.ui.workspace.overlays.region import Bounds
from cranioz.ui.workspace.overlays.top_overlay import (
    TopOverlay,
    TopOverlayError,
)

# ----- Fixtures -----------------------------------------------------


@pytest.fixture
def overlay(qtbot: QtBot) -> TopOverlay:
    ov = TopOverlay()
    qtbot.addWidget(ov.widget())
    return ov


# ----- Identity -----------------------------------------------------


def test_overlay_id_is_stable(overlay: TopOverlay) -> None:
    assert overlay.overlay_id == "overlay.top_bar"


def test_target_region_is_central(overlay: TopOverlay) -> None:
    assert overlay.target_region is Placement.CENTRAL


def test_class_constants_are_accessible() -> None:
    assert TopOverlay.OVERLAY_ID == "overlay.top_bar"
    assert TopOverlay.TARGET_REGION is Placement.CENTRAL
    assert TopOverlay.DEFAULT_HEIGHT == 40
    assert TopOverlay.DEFAULT_MARGIN_X == 8
    assert TopOverlay.DEFAULT_MARGIN_Y == 8


# ----- Widget -------------------------------------------------------


def test_widget_returns_qwidget(overlay: TopOverlay) -> None:
    assert isinstance(overlay.widget(), QWidget)


def test_widget_is_a_qframe(overlay: TopOverlay) -> None:
    assert isinstance(overlay.widget(), QFrame)


def test_widget_is_stable_across_calls(overlay: TopOverlay) -> None:
    assert overlay.widget() is overlay.widget()


def test_widget_has_object_name(overlay: TopOverlay) -> None:
    assert overlay.widget().objectName() == "top_overlay"


def test_widget_has_fixed_height(overlay: TopOverlay) -> None:
    widget = overlay.widget()
    assert widget.minimumHeight() == 40
    assert widget.maximumHeight() == 40


# ----- Reposition ---------------------------------------------------


def test_reposition_sets_geometry_according_to_bounds(
    overlay: TopOverlay,
) -> None:
    bounds = Bounds(x=100.0, y=50.0, width=800.0, height=600.0)
    overlay.reposition(bounds)

    geom = overlay.widget().geometry()
    # x = bounds.x + margin_x = 100 + 8 = 108
    # y = bounds.y + margin_y = 50 + 8 = 58
    # width = bounds.width - 2 * margin_x = 800 - 16 = 784
    # height = fixed_height = 40
    assert geom.x() == 108
    assert geom.y() == 58
    assert geom.width() == 784
    assert geom.height() == 40


def test_reposition_with_origin_bounds(overlay: TopOverlay) -> None:
    bounds = Bounds(x=0.0, y=0.0, width=1000.0, height=800.0)
    overlay.reposition(bounds)

    geom = overlay.widget().geometry()
    assert geom.x() == 8
    assert geom.y() == 8
    assert geom.width() == 1000 - 16
    assert geom.height() == 40


def test_reposition_clamps_negative_width_to_zero(
    overlay: TopOverlay,
) -> None:
    # Region narrower than twice the margin. Expected: width = 0.
    bounds = Bounds(x=0.0, y=0.0, width=10.0, height=100.0)
    overlay.reposition(bounds)

    geom = overlay.widget().geometry()
    assert geom.width() == 0
    assert geom.height() == 40


def test_reposition_is_idempotent(overlay: TopOverlay) -> None:
    bounds = Bounds(x=10.0, y=20.0, width=500.0, height=400.0)
    overlay.reposition(bounds)
    first = overlay.widget().geometry()

    overlay.reposition(bounds)
    second = overlay.widget().geometry()

    assert first == second


def test_reposition_can_be_called_multiple_times(
    overlay: TopOverlay,
) -> None:
    overlay.reposition(Bounds(x=0.0, y=0.0, width=100.0, height=100.0))
    overlay.reposition(Bounds(x=50.0, y=60.0, width=700.0, height=600.0))

    geom = overlay.widget().geometry()
    assert geom.x() == 58
    assert geom.y() == 68
    assert geom.width() == 700 - 16


# ----- Visibility ---------------------------------------------------


def test_overlay_starts_visible(overlay: TopOverlay) -> None:
    assert overlay.is_visible() is True


def test_set_visible_false_hides_widget(overlay: TopOverlay) -> None:
    overlay.set_visible(False)
    assert overlay.is_visible() is False
    assert overlay.widget().isVisible() is False


def test_set_visible_true_shows_widget(overlay: TopOverlay) -> None:
    overlay.set_visible(False)
    overlay.set_visible(True)
    assert overlay.is_visible() is True


def test_visibility_toggle_does_not_destroy_widget(
    overlay: TopOverlay,
) -> None:
    widget = overlay.widget()
    overlay.set_visible(False)
    overlay.set_visible(True)
    assert overlay.widget() is widget


# ----- Custom parameters --------------------------------------------


def test_custom_height(qtbot: QtBot) -> None:
    ov = TopOverlay(height=60)
    qtbot.addWidget(ov.widget())

    assert ov.height == 60
    assert ov.widget().minimumHeight() == 60
    assert ov.widget().maximumHeight() == 60

    ov.reposition(Bounds(x=0.0, y=0.0, width=100.0, height=100.0))
    assert ov.widget().geometry().height() == 60


def test_custom_margins(qtbot: QtBot) -> None:
    ov = TopOverlay(margin_x=20, margin_y=15)
    qtbot.addWidget(ov.widget())

    assert ov.margin_x == 20
    assert ov.margin_y == 15

    ov.reposition(Bounds(x=100.0, y=50.0, width=800.0, height=600.0))
    geom = ov.widget().geometry()
    assert geom.x() == 120  # 100 + 20
    assert geom.y() == 65  # 50 + 15
    assert geom.width() == 800 - 40  # 800 - 2*20


def test_zero_margins(qtbot: QtBot) -> None:
    ov = TopOverlay(margin_x=0, margin_y=0)
    qtbot.addWidget(ov.widget())

    ov.reposition(Bounds(x=10.0, y=20.0, width=100.0, height=100.0))
    geom = ov.widget().geometry()
    assert geom.x() == 10
    assert geom.y() == 20
    assert geom.width() == 100


# ----- Validation ---------------------------------------------------


def test_non_positive_height_raises() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(TopOverlayError, match="height"):
        TopOverlay(height=0)
    with pytest.raises(TopOverlayError, match="height"):
        TopOverlay(height=-1)


def test_negative_margin_x_raises() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(TopOverlayError, match="margin_x"):
        TopOverlay(margin_x=-1)


def test_negative_margin_y_raises() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(TopOverlayError, match="margin_y"):
        TopOverlay(margin_y=-1)


# ----- Content layout -----------------------------------------------


def test_content_layout_returns_qlayout(overlay: TopOverlay) -> None:
    layout = overlay.content_layout()
    assert isinstance(layout, QLayout)


def test_content_layout_is_stable(overlay: TopOverlay) -> None:
    assert overlay.content_layout() is overlay.content_layout()

FILE: tests/unit/ui/workspace/specs/test_area_spec.py

"""Tests for AreaSpec."""

from __future__ import annotations

import pytest

from cranioz.ui.workspace.areas.placement import Placement
from cranioz.ui.workspace.specs.area_spec import AreaSpec, AreaSpecError
from cranioz.ui.workspace.specs.editor_layout_spec import EditorLayoutSpec


def _leaf(editor_id: str) -> EditorLayoutSpec:
    return EditorLayoutSpec.leaf(editor_id)


def test_area_spec_holds_fields() -> None:
    spec = AreaSpec(
        area_id="area.central",
        title="Central",
        placement=Placement.CENTRAL,
        editor_layout=_leaf("editor.a"),
    )
    assert spec.area_id == "area.central"
    assert spec.title == "Central"
    assert spec.placement is Placement.CENTRAL
    assert spec.editor_layout.editor_id == "editor.a"


def test_empty_area_id_raises() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(AreaSpecError, match="area_id"):
        AreaSpec(
            area_id="",
            title="X",
            placement=Placement.CENTRAL,
            editor_layout=_leaf("editor.a"),
        )


def test_empty_title_raises() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(AreaSpecError, match="title"):
        AreaSpec(
            area_id="area.x",
            title="",
            placement=Placement.CENTRAL,
            editor_layout=_leaf("editor.a"),
        )

FILE: tests/unit/ui/workspace/specs/test_editor_layout_spec.py
======================================================================

"""Tests for EditorLayoutSpec."""

from __future__ import annotations

import pytest

from cranioz.ui.workspace.specs.editor_layout_spec import (
    EditorLayoutSpec,
    EditorLayoutSpecError,
    LayoutType,
    SplitDirection,
)

# ----- LEAF ---------------------------------------------------------


def test_leaf_factory_creates_leaf() -> None:
    spec = EditorLayoutSpec.leaf("editor.a")
    assert spec.type is LayoutType.LEAF
    assert spec.editor_id == "editor.a"
    assert spec.direction is None
    assert spec.children == ()


def test_leaf_without_editor_id_raises() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(EditorLayoutSpecError, match="editor_id"):
        EditorLayoutSpec(type=LayoutType.LEAF)


def test_leaf_with_children_raises() -> None:
    child = EditorLayoutSpec.leaf("editor.a")
    with pytest.raises(EditorLayoutSpecError, match="must not have children"):
        EditorLayoutSpec(
            type=LayoutType.LEAF,
            editor_id="editor.b",
            children=(child,),
        )


def test_leaf_with_direction_raises() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(EditorLayoutSpecError, match="must not declare direction"):
        EditorLayoutSpec(
            type=LayoutType.LEAF,
            editor_id="editor.a",
            direction=SplitDirection.HORIZONTAL,
        )


# ----- SPLIT --------------------------------------------------------


def test_split_factory_creates_split() -> None:
    a = EditorLayoutSpec.leaf("editor.a")
    b = EditorLayoutSpec.leaf("editor.b")
    spec = EditorLayoutSpec.split(SplitDirection.HORIZONTAL, (a, b))
    assert spec.type is LayoutType.SPLIT
    assert spec.direction is SplitDirection.HORIZONTAL
    assert spec.children == (a, b)
    assert spec.editor_id is None


def test_split_with_editor_id_raises() -> None:
    a = EditorLayoutSpec.leaf("editor.a")
    b = EditorLayoutSpec.leaf("editor.b")
    with pytest.raises(EditorLayoutSpecError, match="must not declare editor_id"):
        EditorLayoutSpec(
            type=LayoutType.SPLIT,
            direction=SplitDirection.HORIZONTAL,
            children=(a, b),
            editor_id="editor.c",
        )


def test_split_without_direction_raises() -> None:
    a = EditorLayoutSpec.leaf("editor.a")
    b = EditorLayoutSpec.leaf("editor.b")
    with pytest.raises(EditorLayoutSpecError, match="must declare direction"):
        EditorLayoutSpec(
            type=LayoutType.SPLIT,
            children=(a, b),
        )


def test_split_with_one_child_raises() -> None:
    a = EditorLayoutSpec.leaf("editor.a")
    with pytest.raises(EditorLayoutSpecError, match="at least two children"):
        EditorLayoutSpec.split(SplitDirection.HORIZONTAL, (a,))


def test_split_with_zero_children_raises() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(EditorLayoutSpecError, match="at least two children"):
        EditorLayoutSpec.split(SplitDirection.HORIZONTAL, ())


def test_nested_split_is_valid() -> None:
    """Replicates the prototype's 2x2 central area."""
    a = EditorLayoutSpec.leaf("editor.a")
    b = EditorLayoutSpec.leaf("editor.b")
    c = EditorLayoutSpec.leaf("editor.c")
    d = EditorLayoutSpec.leaf("editor.d")

    inner_left = EditorLayoutSpec.split(SplitDirection.VERTICAL, (a, b))
    inner_right = EditorLayoutSpec.split(SplitDirection.VERTICAL, (c, d))
    outer = EditorLayoutSpec.split(SplitDirection.HORIZONTAL, (inner_left, inner_right))

    assert outer.type is LayoutType.SPLIT
    assert len(outer.children) == 2
    assert outer.children[0].type is LayoutType.SPLIT
    assert outer.children[1].type is LayoutType.SPLIT


def test_unknown_layout_type_raises() -> None:
    class Fake:
        pass

    with pytest.raises(EditorLayoutSpecError, match="unknown layout type"):
        EditorLayoutSpec(type=Fake)  # type: ignore[arg-type]



======================================================================
FILE: tests/unit/ui/workspace/specs/test_module_ui_spec.py
======================================================================

"""Tests for ModuleUISpec."""

from __future__ import annotations

import pytest

from cranioz.ui.workspace.areas.placement import Placement
from cranioz.ui.workspace.specs.area_spec import AreaSpec
from cranioz.ui.workspace.specs.editor_layout_spec import EditorLayoutSpec
from cranioz.ui.workspace.specs.module_ui_spec import (
    ModuleUISpec,
    ModuleUISpecError,
)


def _area(area_id: str, placement: Placement, editor_id: str) -> AreaSpec:
    return AreaSpec(
        area_id=area_id,
        title=area_id,
        placement=placement,
        editor_layout=EditorLayoutSpec.leaf(editor_id),
    )


def test_single_central_is_valid() -> None:
    spec = ModuleUISpec(areas=(_area("area.central", Placement.CENTRAL, "editor.a"),))
    assert len(spec.areas) == 1
    assert spec.overlays == ()


def test_left_central_right_is_valid() -> None:
    spec = ModuleUISpec(
        areas=(
            _area("area.left", Placement.LEFT, "editor.left"),
            _area("area.central", Placement.CENTRAL, "editor.central"),
            _area("area.right", Placement.RIGHT, "editor.right"),
        )
    )
    assert len(spec.areas) == 3


def test_four_areas_is_valid() -> None:
    spec = ModuleUISpec(
        areas=(
            _area("area.left", Placement.LEFT, "editor.left"),
            _area("area.central", Placement.CENTRAL, "editor.central"),
            _area("area.right", Placement.RIGHT, "editor.right"),
            _area("area.bottom", Placement.BOTTOM, "editor.bottom"),
        )
    )
    assert len(spec.areas) == 4


def test_without_central_raises() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ModuleUISpecError, match="CENTRAL"):
        ModuleUISpec(
            areas=(
                _area("area.left", Placement.LEFT, "editor.left"),
                _area("area.right", Placement.RIGHT, "editor.right"),
            )
        )


def test_duplicate_area_id_raises() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ModuleUISpecError, match="unique"):
        ModuleUISpec(
            areas=(
                _area("area.central", Placement.CENTRAL, "editor.a"),
                _area("area.central", Placement.CENTRAL, "editor.b"),
            )
        )


def test_empty_areas_raises() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ModuleUISpecError, match="at least one Area"):
        ModuleUISpec(areas=())



======================================================================
FILE: tests/unit/ui/workspace/specs/test_overlay_spec.py
======================================================================

"""Tests for OverlaySpec."""

from __future__ import annotations

import pytest

from cranioz.ui.workspace.areas.placement import Placement
from cranioz.ui.workspace.specs.overlay_spec import (
    OverlaySpec,
    OverlaySpecError,
)


def test_overlay_spec_holds_fields() -> None:
    spec = OverlaySpec(
        overlay_id="overlay.steps",
        title="Steps Panel",
        target_region=Placement.CENTRAL,
    )
    assert spec.overlay_id == "overlay.steps"
    assert spec.title == "Steps Panel"
    assert spec.target_region is Placement.CENTRAL


def test_empty_overlay_id_raises() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(OverlaySpecError, match="overlay_id"):
        OverlaySpec(
            overlay_id="",
            title="X",
            target_region=Placement.CENTRAL,
        )


def test_empty_title_raises() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(OverlaySpecError, match="title"):
        OverlaySpec(
            overlay_id="overlay.x",
            title="",
            target_region=Placement.CENTRAL,
        )
